### [1] [Harmonic and intermodulation emission from direct-to-device satellites - Harmonics of the Starlink 2.6 GHz downlink and the products around them](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14336)
*Balthasar Indermuehle,Benjamin Winkel,Federico Di Vruno,Gyula Jozsa,Liroy Lourenco*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文测量了Starlink直连设备（DTD）卫星下行链路的谐波与互调产物，发现其落入射电天文频段并可能超限，单个卫星合规不能保护射电天文免受星座聚合效应影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DTD卫星搭载移动基站，在陆地移动频段发射，其非线性发射产物可能落入射电天文频段（含被动频段），对射电天文观测构成潜在干扰，因此需要实测并评估其影响。

Method: 利用澳大利亚望远镜致密阵列（ATCA）单天线自相关，在16 cm和4 cm接收机上跟踪DTD及其他卫星过境；测量2620-2630 MHz下行谐波及周围互调产物，进行谐波比率与接收机测试，并模拟星座在Effelsberg和ATCA处的聚合等效功率通量密度。

Result: H2至H4谐波（5240、7860、10480 MHz）每阶约下降16 dB，与仰角无趋势关系，源自卫星本身；H2在距DTD卫星10度以外仍有32%积分被检测到。谐波带内频谱呈载波n重卷积，带外n+2阶及以上互调产物平均低于谐波带19 dB，数秒内在-23至-15 dB间切换；载波附近未检测到高于载波33 dB以下的卫星发射。H2在4 kHz内>= -38.9 dBW，达到或超过SM.329空间站限值；8640颗卫星时，第二和第五谐波聚合分别超出保护标准65和25 dB，2690-2700 MHz带外发射超出>100 dB。

Conclusion: 谐波在服务波束之外仍被接收到，其辐射水平必须直接限制而非通过天线端口功率限制；单颗卫星符合ITU-R限值并不能保护射电天文免受星座聚合效应的影响。

Abstract: Direct-to-device (DTD) satellites carry mobile base stations transmitting in terrestrial mobile bands. Their non-linear emission products can fall into radio astronomy bands, including passive bands. We measure the harmonics of the Starlink DTD downlink at 2620-2630 MHz and the intermodulation products around them, search for products next to the carriers, and compare them with the ITU-R unwanted-emission limits and radio astronomy protection criteria. We use autocorrelations of single Australia Telescope Compact Array antennas on tracked passes of DTD and other satellites with the 16 cm and 4 cm receivers. Harmonic ratios and receiver tests show that the harmonics are satellite-generated. We simulate the aggregate equivalent power flux density of a constellation at Effelsberg and the ATCA. The harmonics H2 to H4 at 5240, 7860 and 10480 MHz fall by about 16 dB per order, show no trend with elevation, and originate on the satellite. H2 is also detected in 32% of integrations more than 10 degrees from any DTD satellite. Inside the harmonic zones the spectra follow the n-fold convolution of the carriers; outside them, intermodulation products of order n+2 and higher lie on average 19 dB below the zone and switch between -23 and -15 dB within seconds. Next to the carriers no satellite emission is detected to 33 dB below the carriers. H2, >=-38.9 dBW in 4 kHz isotropic-equivalent, is at or above the SM.329 space-station limit; H3 and H4 meet it. For 8640 satellites the aggregate exceeds the protection criteria by 65 and 25 dB at the second and fifth harmonics, and by >100 dB for out-of-band emission at the mask level in 2690-2700 MHz. Harmonics are received far outside the serving beams. Their radiated level must be limited directly, not through the power at the antenna port. Compliance with per-satellite ITU-R limits does not protect radio astronomy from the aggregate of a constellation.

</details>


### [2] [ELISA: A Bayesian X/$γ$-ray Spectral Analysis Toolkit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14587)
*Wang-Chen Xue,Sheng-Lun Xie,Chao Zheng,Shao-Lin Xiong,Xiao-Bo Li,Yong Chen,Peng Zhang,Yan-Qiu Zhang,Jia-Cong Liu,Chen-Wei Wang,Wen-Jun Tan,Yue Wang,Jin-Peng Zhang,Zheng-Hang Yu*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: ELISA是一个开源Python工具包，用于X/γ射线前向折叠光谱分析，集成JAX加速的正向模型与统一拟合接口，支持优化与贝叶斯采样，并内置模型验证与比较诊断，在Insight-HXMT蟹状星云观测案例中采样效率显著优于XSPEC默认MCMC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: X/γ射线前向折叠光谱拟合越来越依赖贝叶斯工作流以更好处理计数统计、本底不确定性和复杂参数简并，但完全可复现的端到端实现仍难以在日常分析中直接部署。

Method: 提出ELISA（Efficient Library for Spectral Analysis），一个开源Python工具包，涵盖数据加载、模型构建、推断、模型验证与比较的完整光谱分析闭环；将JAX加速的正向模型与统一拟合接口耦合，同时支持快速优化和现代贝叶斯采样，并内置收敛检查与预测评估诊断。

Result: 在Insight-HXMT蟹状星云观测的光谱拟合示例中，相比XSPEC默认MCMC实现，采样效率获得显著提升。

Conclusion: ELISA为X/γ射线光谱分析提供了可复现、高效且易部署的端到端工具，代码已在GitHub和PyPI公开。

Abstract: Forward-folded X/$γ$-ray spectral fitting increasingly benefits from Bayesian workflows to better capture counting statistics, background uncertainties, and complex parameter degeneracies, while fully reproducible end-to-end implementations are still not always straightforward to deploy in routine analyses. We present ELISA (Efficient Library for Spectral Analysis), an open-source Python toolkit that streamlines the full spectral analysis loop for X/$γ$-ray astronomy: data loading, model construction, inference, and model validation and comparison. ELISA couples a JAX-accelerated forward model with a unified fitting interface, supporting both rapid optimization and modern Bayesian sampling. Built-in diagnostics make convergence checks and predictive assessment a standard part of the workflow, motivating principled model refinement when needed. Using a spectral fitting example of the Insight-HXMT Crab observation, we find substantial improvements in sampling efficiency compared with XSPEC's default MCMC implementation. ELISA is available at https://github.com/wcxve/elisa and on PyPI at https://pypi.org/project/astro-elisa.

</details>


### [3] [Mid-IR Water-Vapor Seeing: Implications for ELT/METIS High-Contrast Imaging from VLTI Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14801)
*Abhirami S Raghu,Prashant Pathak,Olivier Absil,Benjamin Courtney-Barrer,Gilles Orban de Xivry,Julien Woillez,Alexis Matter,Philippe Bério,Roy van Boekel,Bernhard Brandl*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 利用VLTI/GRAVITY的K波段条纹追踪数据，通过群延迟与相位延迟之差测量大气水汽柱密度差异，量化其对中红外高对比度成像波前误差的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接成像与表征岩石系外行星是系外行星科学的核心挑战之一。中红外波段的行星-恒星对比度较可见/近红外更缓和，是理想的高对比度成像窗口，但地面极大望远镜（ELT）在中红外的观测受大气水汽时空涨落严重影响，会引入色散波前误差，且这种误差对工作于短波长的自适应光学系统基本不可见。这对METIS等旨在N波段探测岩石行星热辐射的中红外HCI仪器构成重大挑战，因此需要量化水汽引起的波前误差。

Method: 分析VLTI上GRAVITY仪器的K波段条纹追踪数据，推导群延迟与相位延迟测量之间的光程差，将其作为望远镜上方差分水汽柱密度（Σ）的直接度量。考察多组VLTI配置下差分水汽柱密度RMS（σ_Σ）对可降水量（PWV）、大气质量、视宁度和风速的依赖关系，并探究垂直PWV分布的影响。随后使用功率谱密度分析量化PWV增加如何调制Σ的变率。

Result: 在PWV、大气质量、视宁度与风速等参数中，PWV与σ_Σ相关性最强。PWV增高会调制Σ的变率。该现象在ELT尺度上对应的波前误差约为：L波段15 nm RMS，N波段175 nm RMS（相对K波段）。

Conclusion: 研究为评估与缓解下一代中红外高对比度成像仪器中的大气水汽效应提供了定量框架。所建立的水汽-波前误差定量关系可支撑METIS等中红外HCI仪器的误差预算与校正策略设计。

Abstract: Direct imaging and characterization of rocky exoplanets remains one of the central challenges in exoplanetary science. The mid-infrared (mid-IR) offers a favorable high-contrast imaging (HCI) regime, as the planet-star contrast is more moderate than at shorter wavelengths. However, mid-IR observations with extremely large ground-based telescopes (ELTs) are strongly affected by spatio-temporal fluctuations in atmospheric water vapor (WV), which introduce chromatic wavefront errors that are largely invisible to adaptive optics systems operating at shorter wavelengths. This poses a significant challenge for mid-IR HCI instruments such as METIS, which aims to detect thermal emission from rocky exoplanets in the $N$-band (8--13 $μ$ m). To quantify WV-induced wavefront errors, we analyze $K$-band fringe-tracker data from the GRAVITY instrument at the VLTI and derive the optical path difference between group-delay and phase-delay measurements. This difference provides a direct measure of the differential WV column density ($Σ$) above the telescopes. We examine the dependence of the RMS differential WV column density ($σ_Σ$) on precipitable water vapor (PWV), airmass, seeing, and wind speed across multiple VLTI configurations. Among these parameters, PWV shows the strongest correlation with $σ_Σ$. We further explore the influence of the vertical PWV distribution on this relationship. Next, we use power-spectral-density analysis to quantify how increasing PWV modulates $Σ$ variability. The wavefront error associated with this phenomenon on ELT scale is approximately 15~nm RMS in the $L$-band and 175~nm RMS in the $N$-band, relative to the $K$-band. These results provide a quantitative framework for assessing and mitigating atmospheric WV effects in next-generation mid-IR HCI instruments.

</details>


### [4] [Modelling the Point Spread Function of ILMT images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14819)
*Naveen Dukiya,Bhavya Ailawadhi,Talat Akhunov,Monalisa Dubey,Sara Filali,Paul Hickson,Priyanshi Kumari,Gokul Singh Mehra,Kuntal Misra,Vibhore Negi,Kumar Pranshu,Jean Surdej,Sarvesh Kumar Yadav,Archana Gupta*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出对4米国际液体镜望远镜（ILMT）图像经验点扩散函数（PSF）的建模方法，利用PSFEx对4k×4k图像块进行二阶X-Y变异性建模，成功再现了PSF及其在视场中的变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ILMT的PSF受液体镜特性、仪器及大气因素影响；由于采用TDI跟踪模式，PSF沿赤纬轴因差动运动和星轨弯曲而变化，5元校正器只能部分均匀化，且PSF沿赤经方向随天空条件变化。因此需要对其PSF进行精确建模。

Method: 使用PSFEx软件对4k×4k图像块进行PSF建模，考虑二阶X-Y空间变异性，以刻画PSF在整个视场中的变化。

Result: 所开发的软件能够稳健地再现PSF及其在整个视场内的变化。

Conclusion: 该建模方法可成功描述ILMT图像的经验PSF及其空间变化特性。

Abstract: We have devised an approach to model the empirical Point Spread Function (PSF) of the images taken with the 4-meter International Liquid Mirror Telescope (ILMT). The characteristics of the ILMT PSF are influenced by the nature of the Liquid Mirror, as well as by instrument specific and atmospheric factors. As the telescope uses Time Delay Integration (TDI) mode for tracking the passing objects, the PSF varies along the declination axis due to differential motion and star-trail curvature. The 5-element optical corrector homogenizes the PSF to some degree, but does not completely eliminate the disturbing effects mentioned above. The PSF in the RA direction is changing due to the sky conditions. We show that we can successfully model the PSF of 4k$\times$4k chunks using PSFEx, accounting for second-order X-Y variability. Our software robustly reproduces the PSF and its variation across the field.

</details>


### [5] [From Sky to Science: Time Domain Astronomy with the ILMT Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14827)
*Kuntal Misra,Bhavya Ailawadhi,Talat Akhunov,Monalisa Dubey,Naveen Dukiya,Sara Filali,Paul Hickson,Priyanshi Kumari,Gokul Singh Mehra,Vibhore Negi,Kumar Pranshu,Jean Surdej,Sarvesh Kumar Yadav*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: PyLMT瞬变检测流水线自2023年11月以来在ILMT图像中近实时探测到约1万个瞬变源和变源，展示了该巡天在多种天体物理现象研究中的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 介绍ILMT巡天及其PyLMT流水线的运行情况，并强调该巡天对广泛天体物理源的潜在贡献，以展示其科学价值。

Method: 基于PyLMT瞬变检测流水线，对ILMT图像进行近实时处理，探测瞬变源和变源。

Result: 自2023年11月以来探测到约1万个瞬变源和变源，涵盖超新星、变星、活动星系核（AGN）光变以及小行星、彗星等太阳系天体，呈现出广泛的物理现象多样性。

Conclusion: ILMT巡天具备研究超新星、AGN、变星和小行星等代表性科学目标的潜力，可对广泛类别的天体物理源作出贡献。

Abstract: The PyLMT transient detection pipeline has been operational since November 2023 and has detected $\sim$10,000 transients and variable objects in ILMT images in near real time. The survey has enabled the discovery and characterisation of a range of bright and faint optical transients. The detected population spans a broad diversity of astrophysical phenomena, including supernovae, variable stars, active galactic nuclei (AGN) variability, and Solar System objects such as asteroids and comets. We provide a concise overview of the detection of a variety of transient and variable classes identified with the ILMT, emphasising the survey's potential contributions to a broad class of astrophysical sources. We highlight representative science cases, including supernovae, AGNs, variable stars, and asteroids, to demonstrate the scientific potential of the ILMT survey.

</details>


### [6] [Occultation polarimetry as a probe of unresolved polarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15811)
*A. G. Tevzadze*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文建立了掩星偏振测量学的通用理论框架，从未分辨的全斯托克斯光变曲线中直接提取空间偏振混合的模型无关定量约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未分辨源的低偏振可能源于局部弱偏振，也可能源于强偏振区域之间的空间相消，二者在观测上难以区分。掩星过程中遮挡边界移动可对源进行差异化采样，从而打破这种空间简并。

Method: 针对平稳、空间非相干的源在单调掩星情形下，将斯托克斯参量变化与任意弯曲遮挡边界所采样的偏振联系起来，并对归一化斯托克斯矢量进行精确的方差分解。

Result: 偏振亏损可精确分解为三个非负贡献：局部偏振亏损、临边样本内混合、以及临边样本平均偏振态之间的混合。最后一项可直接测量，并给出总空间混合的模型无关下界；相关协方差张量进一步刻画偏振变化的方向与相关性，包括线偏振与圆偏振之间的相关性。有限采样仍保持该下界，并可通过互补掩星轨迹加强。

Conclusion: 掩星偏振测量学是一种强有力的观测技术，能够从未分辨斯托克斯光变曲线中提取关于空间偏振混合的定量、模型无关约束，揭示在现有角分辨率下原本不可及的偏振结构范围与形态。

Abstract: Context: Low polarization in an unresolved source may reflect weak local polarization or spatial cancellation between strongly polarized regions. An occultation can break this spatial degeneracy by differentially sampling the source as the occulting boundary moves. Aims: We develop a general theoretical framework for occultation polarimetry and determine what information can be inferred directly from unresolved full Stokes light curves, without reconstructing a polarization image or prescribing a source model. Methods: For a stationary, spatially incoherent source undergoing monotonic occultation, we relate Stokes variations to the polarization sampled by an arbitrarily curved occulting boundary and derive an exact variance decomposition of the normalized Stokes vector. Results: The polarization deficit decomposes exactly into three non-negative contributions: local polarization deficit, mixing within limb samples, and mixing between their mean polarization states. The last is directly measurable and provides a sharp, model-independent lower bound on total spatial mixing. The associated covariance tensor further characterizes the direction and correlations of the resolved polarization variations, including those between linear and circular polarization. Finite sampling preserves the bound, which can be strengthened through complementary occultation trajectories. Conclusions: Occultation polarimetry provides a powerful observational technique for extracting quantitative, model independent constraints on spatial polarization mixing directly from unresolved Stokes light curves. It offers a means to reveal both the extent and the form of polarization structure otherwise inaccessible at the available angular resolution.

</details>


### [7] [The Long-Term Radial Velocity Stability of the COS G130M/1291 Mode](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15819)
*John Debes,David M. French,Leonardo A. Dos Santos,Travis Fischer,Svea Hernandez,Ravi Sankrit*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 利用标准白矮星WD 0308-565检验COS远紫外模式17年径向速度稳定性，证明可达<3 km/s（15年）及600 m/s（高信噪比）精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨哈勃太空望远镜COS仪器远紫外模式在长期运行中的径向速度稳定性，为空间紫外精密视向速度测量开辟新方向，并为未来高分辨率紫外光谱仪（如宜居世界天文台）提供参考。

Method: 以标准白矮星WD 0308-565为参照目标，利用覆盖COS约17年运行寿命中大部分时间的观测数据，评估其远紫外模式的长期径向速度稳定性，并讨论观测策略与适合的候选天体。

Result: 在中等信噪比下，COS在15年基线内可实现<3 km/s的径向速度稳定性；在更高信噪比和较少观测历元时，可达σ_V=600 m/s；该目标的绝对径向速度精度与地基光学光谱结果一致。

Conclusion: COS可用于精密径向速度测量，为空间紫外天文学开辟新的科学方向；随着COS运行近20年，该工作也预示了未来高分辨率紫外光谱仪在精密视向速度方面的潜力。

Abstract: We investigate the long-term radial velocity (RV) stability of one of the Far-UV (FUV) modes on the Cosmic Origins Spectrograph (COS). We do so by using a standard white dwarf, WD~0308-565, that has been observed over a large fraction of COS's operational lifetime of 17 years. We demonstrate that for modest signal-to-noise COS can return an RV stability of $<$3 km s$^{-1}$ over a baseline of 15 years. For higher SNR and a smaller number of epochs, we obtain $σ_{\mathrm V}=600$ m s$^{-1}$. We also show that the absolute radial velocity accuracy for this target is consistent with results from ground-based optical spectroscopy. We discuss some observational strategies and astrophysical objects that may be well suited to pushing to the very RV limit of the instrument. Utilizing COS for precision RV work opens a new scientific avenue for space-based UV astronomy as COS approaches 20 years of operations and hints at what may be possible with the stability of a high-resolution UV spectrograph on future missions such as the Habitable Worlds Observatory.

</details>


### [8] [The Simons Observatory: Frequency-resolved beam calibration for the SO SATs with POLOCALC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15913)
*Nadia Dachlythra,Gabriele Coppi,Ludovico Bizzarri,Federico Nati,Alessandro Novelli,Elle C. Shaw,Samuel Day-Weiss,Federico Astori,Susanna Azzoni,Dillon J. Bass,Artem Basyrov,Federico Cacciotti,Rolando Dünner Planella,Nicholas Galitzki,Remington Gerras,John C. Groh,Jon E. Gudmundsson,Saianeesh K. Haridas,Kathleen Harrington,Carlos Hervías-Caimapo,Bradley R. Johnson,Brian Keating,Adrian T. Lee,Anto I. Lonappan,Noemi Mezzanzanica,Lyman Page,Giulia Rancati Cattaneo,Sara M. Simon,Thierry Souverin,Yoshinori Sueno,Edward J. Wollack,Kyohei Yamada,Mario Zannoni*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文利用无人机搭载的90 GHz单频源，对西蒙斯天文台小孔径望远镜（SAT）进行远场波束标定，并与行星观测得到的带平均波束进行对比，验证了频率分辨标定方案的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确表征望远镜波束的频率依赖行为对追踪光学非理想性至关重要，而仅依靠带平均行星波束难以捕捉这些信息。本文旨在检验一种频率分辨的波束标定方案是否可行，并评估重建带平均波束所需的频率分辨率。

Method: 使用无人机搭载的90 GHz单频源进行扫描测量，获得两台SAT的远场波束；将2025年4月和12月两次观测的结果，与SAT行星观测得到的带平均波束进行对比。通过在不同频点上测量波束响应，对系统的频率依赖行为建模，并合成带平均无人机波束与真实行星带平均波束比较。

Result: 两台SAT在90 GHz附近的波束基本符合衍射极限光学所预期的频率缩放规律，仅其中一台望远镜最近旁瓣存在适度偏差。两台望远镜中心晶片的合成无人机带平均波束与真实行星带平均波束一致，在ℓ<500的多极矩范围内平均偏差小于4%。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了频率分辨波束标定方案的可行性，所测波束遵循衍射极限缩放，且合成带平均波束与行星观测结果高度一致。研究同时指出该对比仅基于平均产物，其目的是验证方法而非确定精确的波束协方差和仪器要求。

Conclusion: 日本天文学与天体物理学在机构层面确有扩张，但并非参与度的完全均衡化，而是领先机构核心持续存在条件下的参与机构基础扩展。

Abstract: Using bibliometric data from publication records, we investigate the institutional broadening of Japanese astronomy and astrophysics over 1996-2025. The number of Japan-affiliated publications increased from 588 to 2,156, and the number of unique participating Japanese institutions increased from 123 to 227, indicating a nominal broadening of the institutional base. However, institution-publication links remained concentrated among leading institutions, and the effective number of institutions, a frequency-weighted diversity measure, increased more gradually than the number of unique participating institutions. These results show that Japanese astronomy and astrophysics broadened institutionally, but not through a complete equalization of institutional participation. The observed change is best interpreted as an expansion of the participating institutional base around a persistent core of leading institutions.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 表面亮度与点源探测极限随曝光时间增加而变深，大致符合背景限标度关系；当前条件下COSMOS-8K通常达到更深的表面亮度极限（尤其在红光滤光片），而QHY461像素更小、对望远镜PSF采样更好。连续谱减除成功揭示了M106的Hα与[O III]结构以及天鹅座环的纤维状结构；校正后M106的Hα中值比值为0.973。

Abstract: Wide-field narrowband imaging provides an efficient approach for mapping low-surface-brightness line emission over large angular scales. We present initial performance tests of Multi-Application Survey Telescope Array (MASTA) for narrowband imaging. Using commissioning data obtained with the QHY461 and COSMOS-8K cameras, we characterize the basic reduction procedure, photometric calibration, image quality, and point-source detection limits. The measured surface-brightness and point-source detection limits become deeper with increasing exposure time, roughly following the expected background-limited scaling. Under the current observing and reduction conditions, the COSMOS-8K camera generally reaches deeper surface-brightness limits, particularly in the red filters, while QHY461 has smaller pixel size and provides better sampling of the telescope point spread function (PSF). As demonstrations, we perform continuum subtraction for the nearby galaxy M106 and the supernova remnant Cygnus Loop using adjacent narrowband filters and reveal the Hα and [O III] structures in M106 and filamentary structures in the Cygnus Loop. We further compare the MASTA surface brightness Hα map of M106 with an independent SITELLE measurement. After accounting for [N II] contamination and the wavelength-dependent response of the 6544 Å filter in the f /1.7 converging beam, the corrected median ratio is 0.973. These tests indicate that MASTA is a promising facility for wide-field narrowband imaging. Further improvements in calibration frames, including flux calibration, field-dependent bandpass characterization, and spatially varying PSF modeling and matching will be required for quantitative studies of diffuse ionized gas.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: T-matrix形式化方法能最优地解决化学与结构高度复杂粒子的辐射散射和吸收问题，但其应用于大量组分构成的粒子模型时，长期受限于庞大的计算负担。因此，在粒子不对称性和非均匀性起重要作用的情况下，研究光散射与吸收过程通常不得不依赖简化假设，这影响了透射光谱和消光曲线的正确解读。该问题在多个科学领域影响巨大，尤其在天体物理学中至关重要，因为对高度非均匀粒子建模是理解星际介质（ISM）各相消光作用、以及探测太阳系内外行星大气光传输物理的基本要求。

Method: 本文描述了一种新的T-matrix形式化实现的应用，该实现基于Borghese、Denti和Saija最初开发的框架，命名为Nano-particle Transition Matrix code。它利用现代硬件架构的计算能力，来降低与求解星际介质尘埃及热木星类行星大气气溶胶相关粒子模型有关的计算成本。

Result: 该新实现能够处理日益复杂的计算，并直接影响到对詹姆斯·韦布空间望远镜（JWST）产生的凌日系外行星透射光谱、以及大气遥感红外系外行星大调查（ARIEL）预期光谱的解读。

Conclusion: 新的T-matrix实现通过利用现代硬件架构显著降低了复杂粒子模型的计算成本，使处理更高复杂度的计算成为可能，从而为星际介质尘埃研究和系外行星（如热木星）大气气溶胶研究提供支持，并对JWST和ARIEL等任务的透射光谱解读产生直接影响。

Abstract: The Transition Matrix formalism (T-matrix) offers an optimal solution to the problem of radiation scattering and absorption by particles characterized by a high degree of chemical and structural complexity. However, its application to particle models composed by large numbers of constituents has been classically limited by the substantial computational burden of the method. As a consequence, the investigation of light scattering and absorption processes, in cases where particle asymmetry and non-homogeneity play an important role, typically need to rely on simplifying assumptions which affect the correct interpretation of transmission spectra and extinction curves. This problem has a huge impact on many fields of scientific application. In particular, it has a critical role in astrophysics, because the ability to model highly non homogeneous particles is a fundamental requirement to understand the role of extinction in different phases of the interstellar medium (ISM), as well as to probe the physics of light transmission in planetary atmospheres within the Solar System and beyond. In this work we describe the application of a new implementation of the T-matrix formalism, based on the framework originally developed by Borghese, Denti & Saija and named Nano-particle Transition Matrix code, which leverages the power of modern hardware architectures to reduce the computational costs related to the solution of relevant particle models for the investigation of dust in the ISM and of hot-Jupyter class planets atmospheric aerosols. This new implementation of the T-matrix formalism can handle calculations of increasing complexity with a direct impact in the interpretation of the transmission spectra of transiting exoplanets produced by the James Webb Space Telescope (JWST) and of those expected from the Atmospheric Remote-sensing Infrared Exoplanet Large survey (ARIEL).

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时域巡天常产生跨不规则观测时刻与不同测光波段的异步图像序列，许多应用（如瞬时源需尽早识别以触发跟进观测）要求每次新观测后即可预测。强引力透镜超新星跟进时间极其关键，是这一设定的典型例子，因此需要一个能因果地、实时进行分类的可复用架构。

Method: 将问题形式化为OASIS分类，探索可复用Transformer架构做因果实时分类。对比方法与Bag等人的循环基线（ConvLSTM处理图像+独立LSTM编码观测时刻，并把异步观测映射到固定"波段×观测"网格、未观测波段填零）。在统一训练设置下比较两种建模时空token交互的Transformer方案：因子化注意力（分离空间与时间交互）与联合注意力（同时建模）。引入选择性读出。

Result: 在示范数据集上，因子化架构取得平均损失0.095–0.100，循环基线为0.128，联合架构为0.107或更高。选择性读出进一步将最佳损失降至0.089（因子化模型）。

Conclusion: 结果表明因子化注意力更适合该数据集，但并不意味着架构具有普适优越性。作者将这些架构作为可复用基础发布，供时域天文学及其他涉及演化空间特征且需实时预测的应用使用。

Abstract: Time-domain surveys often produce asynchronous image sequences across irregular observation times and photometric bands. In many applications, predictions are needed after each new observation, particularly when transient sources must be identified early to trigger follow-up. Strongly lensed supernovae (LSNe), for which follow-up is particularly time-critical, provide a useful example. We formulate this setting as On-the-fly ASynchronous Image Stream (OASIS) classification and explore reusable transformer architectures for causal, on-the-fly classification. We demonstrate these architectures on the lensed-transient classification problem studied by Bag et al. (2026a,b), distinguishing LSNe Ia from unlensed astrophysical transients. We compare them with the recurrent baseline adopted by Bag et al. (2026b), which combines a ConvLSTM branch for image processing with a separate LSTM branch for encoding observation times. The baseline maps asynchronous observations onto a fixed band-by-observation grid, filling unobserved bands with zeros. Using a common training setup, we compare two transformer-based approaches to modeling interactions among spatio-temporal tokens: factorized attention, which separates spatial and temporal interactions, and joint attention, which models them together. On the demonstration dataset, factorized architectures achieve mean losses of $0.095$--$0.100$, compared with $0.128$ for the recurrent baseline and $0.107$ or higher for joint architectures. A selective readout further reduces the best loss to $0.089$ for a factorized model. These results suggest that factorized attention suits this dataset better, without implying universal architectural superiority. We release the architectures as reusable foundations for time-domain astronomy and other applications involving evolving spatial features that require on-the-fly predictions.

</details>


### [14] [Estimating the spectral resolution of a radio data cube from its noise properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17223)
*P. Serra,G. Mulas,K. M. Hess,P. Kamphuis,T. Westmeier,Y. Ascasibar,J. M. van der Hulst*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出一种从噪声特性估计射电数据立方体光谱分辨率的新方法，基于噪声自相关与卷积核功率谱的解析关系，并开发了鲁棒算法，已集成到开源工具 spectre 中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确估计数据立方体的光谱分辨率对于数据分析至关重要，但传统方法可能需要外部信息或特定校准源；作者希望直接从数据本身（噪声特性）实现分辨率估计。

Method: 假设数据立方体是光谱无相关立方体与恒定卷积核 K 的卷积结果；利用从立方体提取的噪声光谱的自相关与核功率谱之间的解析关系估计 K 及其积分（即分辨率）；针对有限光谱数/通道数、真实信号和伪影等现实情形，设计了多种算法增强鲁棒性。

Result: 在一系列文献数据集上验证了方法有效性；该方法适用于满足假设的任意数据立方体，不受观测频率或第三轴物理含义限制，例如可用于瞬变搜索数据立方体的时间分辨率测量。

Conclusion: 提出的方法可从噪声特性稳健估计光谱（或时间）分辨率，具有通用性，并已作为开源软件工具 spectre 的一部分公开可用。

Abstract: We present a new method to estimate the spectral resolution of a radio data cube from its noise properties. We assume that the data cube is the result of the convolution of a spectrally-uncorrelated cube with a constant convolution kernel K. We demonstrate that K and the resolution, defined as K's integral, can be estimated thanks to the analytical relation between the autocorrelation of the noise spectra extracted from the cube and the power spectrum of the kernel. We discuss a number of algorithms that make this method robust in the realistic case of a finite number of spectra and channels, real signal and artefacts, and we present the results obtained for a number of datasets from the literature. We further highlight that this method is applicable to any data cube that meets our assumptions independently of the observing frequency or the exact nature of the cube's "third" axis, e.g., it could be used to measure the time resolution of transient-search data cubes. We make this method available as part of the open-source software tool spectre.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 太阳暗条在演化过程中因视线投影效应呈现碎片化与合并等复杂形态与拓扑变化，导致同一结构在不同时刻外观不同，自动化追踪难度大，需要解决碎片关联问题。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提方法在暗条碎片关联任务上取得可靠性能。

Abstract: Solar filaments are prominent features in H$α$ observations. During their evolution, they often exhibit complex morphological and topological changes, which, due to line-of-sight projection effects, appear as fragmentation and merging. These phenomena make it difficult to reliably track solar filaments using automated methods, as the same structure may appear different over time. To address this issue, we formulate filament fragment association as a graph-based learning problem, where individual fragments are represented as nodes and candidate pairwise relationships are modeled as edges to infer whether fragments belong to the same filament. Furthermore, we propose a novel graph neural network (GNN) that incorporates multiplicative aggregation layers to enhance feature aggregation in the graph, replacing conventional additive aggregation with a multiplicative mechanism to better capture information between fragments. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method achieves reliable performance in filament fragment association, validating the effectiveness of the graph-based formulation and the proposed multiplicative aggregation mechanism.

</details>

Abstract: Accurate forecasting of optical turbulence (OT) is useful to optimise telescope scheduling and maximising observing efficiency, as the performance of adaptive optics systems strongly depends on turbulence conditions. While various numerical weather prediction and machine learning models are developed to predict these complex turbulence properties, properly evaluating their added value requires well-defined baseline reference models. This study establishes baseline performance thresholds for key astroclimatic parameters using two distinct models: one forecasting the parameter average over a specific timescale, and the other forecasting the temporal evolution over the same specific timescale. We apply these frameworks to extensive, multi-year datasets from the sites of the Very Large Telescope (VLT) and the Large Binocular Telescope (LBT) and discuss the possibility of extrapolating reference values of forecast performances for other sites based on their optical turbulence statistical characteristics. Our results show that baseline predictive accuracy varies according to the observing site, the specific parameter, the chosen timescale, and the forecasting approach. Therefore, an OT forecasting model's accuracy cannot be quantified in absolute terms, but relatively to the baseline.

Abstract: Standard planetary radio science analyses may be inadequate for accurately interpreting data from the Hera mission at the Didymos system due to the anticipated challenge posed by the coupling between the translational and rotational motions of the asteroids. This work presents a covariance analysis of the Hera radio science experiment using this advanced model. The tested dynamical scenarios are based on recent lightcurve observations indicating that Dimorphos is in an excited non-principal axis spin state that is, on average, tidally locked. Three non-principal axis scenarios are analyzed using a multi-arc local estimation approach instead of the global one adopted in previous investigations involving non-chaotic states. The results show that tumbling or chaotic cases significantly improve the recovery of the bodies' moments of inertia due to the enhanced observability of the system's dynamics from the excited spin state. However, the local estimation approach slightly degrades the accuracy of the mass estimation by a factor of 1.3-3 and Dimorphos' relative orbit by about one order of magnitude. Developing a dedicated constrained multi-arc filter is thus required to improve the estimation accuracy of the Didymos system's dynamical state further. Nevertheless, these results still meet the Hera radio science requirements.

Abstract: The European Space Agency's Hera mission aims to advance planetary defense by investigating the Didymos binary asteroid system following NASA's DART impact on Dimorphos. This data enable more precise estimation of key parameters such as asteroid masses, rotational states, and moments of inertia, with up to 90% uncertainty reduction in Dimorphos' dynamical state under chaotic scenarios. Sensitivity analysis reveals the influence of pointing accuracy, image noise, and imaging frequency. Additionally, dedicated scientific investigations using Dimorphos-focused CubeSat images further advance understanding of the system. The photometric data with the correction for rotational phases, can be explained by a blackbody temperature of $328\pm7$ K, consistent with the nucleus surface temperature empirically estimated at 1.09 au from the Sun (327 K). This blackbody approximation for the nucleus leads to $a = 3.6^{+0.5}_{-0.3}$ km and $b = 1.6^{+0.2}_{-0.2}$ km by assuming an emissivity of unity. The observed low-resolution N-band spectrum showed an overall smooth continuum with no prominent excess emission feature at $\sim$10 $μ$m due to fine silicate grains in the cometary coma. Abstract: We develop accelerated three-dimensional simulations of growing planet pairs that draw on a finite gas reservoir. An annulus compression (Scaling A) shortens the predicted clearing time and shrinks the computational domain at equal grid resolution while preserving key physical parameters. Planetary seeds start with Hill radii equal to the disk scale height, retaining three-dimensional accretion. We demonstrate the method in scaled PDS 70 and Solar-System configurations, assuming perfect Hill capture by growing planets on circular orbits. We compare Scaling A at radius-compression factor \(Γ=1.75\) with the unscaled PDS system at matched resolution, through a 1\% reduction of the interplanetary gas inventory. The scaled run is nearly an order of magnitude faster, with similar accreted mass and a comparable partition of gas between the planets. For the Solar-System configuration at \(Γ=3\), the scaling forecasts a speedup of nearly two orders of magnitude at equal local resolution. The scaling is complementary to orbital advection. On eight CPU cores of a desktop computer, the scaled PDS 70 and Solar-System runs reduce the gas inventory between the orbiting planets by roughly half in less than a day. Accretion-driven orbital convergence narrows the shared feeding zone and, together with gas clearing, limits the planetary growth budget. In our scaled PDS 70 and Solar-System runs, the angular momentum carried by meridional inflow directly constrains delivery theory. These results establish a practical route to testing accretion theory against simulations at matched scaled parameters, before applying it to unscaled planetary systems.

The formation of km sized planetesimals is the most challenging stage, where radial drift and fragmentation rapidly remove meter- sized solids from the protoplanetary disc. Circumbinary discs are much less efficient, with capture rates dropping to around 1 percent of the single-star case for binaries above 1 au and becoming negligible for large planetesimals at wider separations. By combining the two complementary approaches, we successfully detect a He\,I triplet absorption feature of $1.10 \pm 0.22\%$ ($5.1σ$). The signal exhibits a velocity shift consistent with zero and is attributed to the exoplanetary atmosphere. An injection test reveals that the PCA-based method significantly suppresses the retrieved signal. Additionally, using the ATES and TPM codes, we modeled the atmospheric photoevaporation and the helium triplet profile for different He/H ratios and stellar XUV fluxes, accounting for instrument and planetary rotational broadening. From our simulations we derive a mass-loss rate and an evaporation efficiency for KELT-18b of $10.47 \leq \log_{10}(\dot{M}) \leq 11.68$ and $0.153 \leq η\leq 0.321$, demonstrating that the atmosphere of KELT-18b is undergoing photoevaporation. Furthermore, our simulations show good agreement with the observed transmission spectrum, allowing us to exclude very low He/H ratios at a $3σ$ confidence level for XUV fluxes up to approximately twice the nominal reconstructed value. Abstract: The mechanism driving the evolution and dispersal of protoplanetary discs has a substantial impact on the theories of planet formation. To date, neither the viscous paradigm nor the MHD wind-driven framework have been ruled out by the observations. The statistical richness of the observed samples provides a unique opportunity to test evolutionary models through population synthesis. However, comparisons between models and observations are normally carried out by eye, and a more statistically rigorous approach has not yet been implemented. We present DEMOS, a Bayesian inference tool that aims to infer the model parameters and initial conditions that best reproduce the full distribution of global properties of disc populations. This framework relies on a comparison between observations and synthetic populations generated with the 1D disc population-synthesis code Diskpop. We tested the tool with both the MHD wind model and the viscous framework with internal photoevaporation. Applying DEMOS to synthetic populations, we find that, for both models, it accurately recovers the model parameters of disc populations and their associated uncertainties, including cases in which initial conditions are correlated with the stellar mass. In the optimal scenario in which disc masses, accretion rates and disc sizes are available, the tool can also constrain the angular-momentum transport parameter. We show that, although parameter estimates are the most precise with samples of about 100 discs, the tool also yields meaningful constraints for smaller populations of 10-30 discs. We further find that the predictions obtained with DEMOS closely align with those found by previous works. We anticipate that DEMOS will be a valuable tool to infer the initial conditions of observed disc populations, to design observational programmes, and to assess which evolutionary model best reproduces the data.

Abstract: Assessing whether disc evolution is driven by viscosity or MHD winds is crucial to determine the conditions in which planets form. The rich statistical information encoded in nearby star-forming regions provides an excellent opportunity to probe disc evolution. For the viscous-photoevaporative model, the typical wind mass-loss rate is $\dot{M}_\text{w} \sim 10^{-9}$ M$_\odot$ yr$^{-1}$, scaling with $M_\star$ with an exponent of $\sim 2.5$. The inferred mean age is 1.6 Myr for the MHD winds model and 0.9 Myr for the viscous-photoevaporative model. Despite a slight preference for the latter, Bayesian evidence shows that both models reproduce the data comparably well, and neither can be ruled out. However, their best-fit synthetic populations predict substantially different evolutionary pathways. Unlike other proposals, there is no need to invoke any reservoir of planetesimals or external perturbers --- the chain unravels from within. Abstract: Binaries composed of a luminous OB star and a black hole (BH) are a key evolutionary link between massive stellar binaries and merging compact-object binaries, but only a handful are known and their occurrence fraction remains poorly constrained. Result: 测量的密度和波传播方向可由发射率加权的视线量较好代表；但相速度并不只受发射率加权控制，波速振幅的视线分布也起重要作用。因此，推断的磁场更宜用发射率加权密度结合振幅依赖的有效速度来表示。发射率加权在大部分视场仍是合理近似和有用基准，尤其是在视线未穿过强结构等离子片状区域处。

Abstract: Two-dimensional (2D) coronal seismology based on coronagraphic imaging spectroscopy has emerged as a promising method for mapping the coronal magnetic field, but the physical meaning of its inferred quantities under optically thin line-of-sight (LOS) integration remains uncertain. We investigate this problem by synthesizing coronagraphic observables from a global wave-driven magnetohydrodynamic model with a long LOS extent and a structured corona, and by applying the same analysis procedures used for observations. We compare the diagnosed density, wave propagation direction, phase speed, and magnetic-field strength with different LOS-weighted reference quantities from the model. We find that the measured density and the wave propagation direction are both well represented by LOS emissivity-weighted quantities. In contrast, the phase speed is not controlled by emissivity weighting alone: the LOS distribution of wave velocity amplitude also plays an important role. As a result, the inferred magnetic field is better represented by combining the emissivity-weighted density with an amplitude-dependent effective speed. Emissivity weighting nevertheless remains a reasonable approximation, and a useful baseline, over much of the field of view, especially where the LOS does not cross strongly structured plasma-sheet-like regions. These results clarify the physical interpretation of 2D coronal seismology and provide guidance for future coronal magnetic-field tomography and model-constrained applications.

Abstract: Recently, an analog to the t = 0 moment of our Solar System's meteoritic record was identified for the first time around the Class I protostar HOPS-315. Crystalline silicates were found to recondense from SiO gas that cools as it advects upward from a hot thermostat region in the inner midplane. This t=0 moment may be associated with the formation of the first planetesimals in this disk. We explore whether HOPS-68, a Class I protostar with similar properties and system geometries, is engaged in such refractory recondensation as well. A detection of cooler gas-phase SiO in absorption above a hot disk midplane would allow us to achieve this goal, thanks to a previous detection of crystalline silicates. HOPS-68 was observed with the JWST MIRI and NIRSpec, showing multiple bands of molecular absorption. We use local thermal equilibrium slab models to measure the temperature, column density, velocity, and radial location of these bands. The molecular absorption bands of gaseous SiO, H2O, CO2, and 13CO trace a warm molecular inversion layer (300-500 K) at mid-altitudes above the hot disk midplane from 0.3-16 AU, which is consistently blue-shifted with respect to the system velocity by 33-53 km/s. Atacama Large Millimeter/submillimeter Array (ALMA) observations of HOPS-68 kinematically resolve an SiO-rich jet and wind. We confirm that HOPS-68, like HOPS-315, is undergoing an epoch analogous to the t=0 moment in the Solar System's planet formation timeline. The detected warm molecular layer traces the base of a disk wind, which carries sublimated material from the thermostat region near 2 AU up into the outer disk. As for HOPS-315, the molecular layer in the inner few AU of HOPS-68 is enhanced in bulk oxygen as a result of this sublimation. Since HOPS-68 is less evolved than HOPS-315, this may indicate a prolonged epoch in protostellar evolution in which such oxygen enhancement occurs.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重联型日冕加热模型预测磁场复杂性（非势性、电流、剪切）应独立于总磁通量增强日冕辐射，但Fisher et al. (1998)未发现复杂性独立贡献的证据，本文拟用更优数据重新检验该问题。

Method: 使用SDO/HMI矢量磁图（SHARP的16个磁场参数，表征场强、梯度、电流、螺度与非势性）和SDO/AIA的304Å、193Å、94Å及孤立Fe XVIII波段成像，测量137个活动区在约0.09、1.4、7.1 MK的过渡区与日冕辐射；采用偏相关分析，在控制总无符号垂直磁通后分离各复杂性参数的贡献。

Result: 无符号磁通Phi_M解释辐射方差的72–82%，符合幂律I~Phi_M^α（α=0.61–1.19），与既往软X射线结果一致。偏相关显示部分垂直电流和磁场参数对304Å过渡区辐射有弱正贡献（r=0.2–0.3），但其他活动区平均复杂性指标对日冕辐射增强证据不足。例外是总光球磁自由能密度rho_tot，与Fe XVIII辐射呈正偏相关（r=0.49, p<0.001），但与其他AIA波段的偏相关为负或无。

Conclusion: 准稳态日冕加热主要随磁通量定标，而rho_tot可能对高温日冕加热有贡献；在较低温度下，储能高于其磁通预期值的区域反而产生比预期更少的EUV辐射。

Abstract: Reconnection-based models of coronal heating predict that magnetic complexity---non-potential configurations, currents, shear---should enhance coronal emission independently of total magnetic flux. Previous work, however, found no evidence that complexity contributes independently to coronal heating (Fisher et al. 1998). We revisit this question using SDO/HMI vector magnetograms and SDO/AIA EUV imaging. HMI Space-weather Active Region Patches (SHARPs) provide 16 magnetic parameters characterizing field strength, gradients, currents, helicity, and non-potentiality. We measure spatially-integrated transition region and coronal emission using AIA 304A, 193A, 94A, and isolated Fe XVIII emission for 137 active regions, sampling plasma at ~0.09MK, ~1.4MK, and ~7.1MK. Partial correlation analysis isolates the contribution of each complexity parameter after controlling for total unsigned vertical magnetic flux. Unsigned flux (Phi_M) explains 72--82\% of variance in emission, following power laws (I ~ Phi_M^alpha, where alpha=0.61-1.19) consistent with prior soft X-ray results. Partial correlations reveal weak positive contributions (r = 0.2-0.3) to transition region (304A) emission from some vertical current and field parameters, but little evidence that other active-region-averaged measures of magnetic complexity enhance coronal emission. The exception is total photospheric magnetic free energy density (rho_tot), which shows a positive partial correlation (r = 0.49, p < 0.001) with Fe XVIII emission, but negative, or non-existent, partial correlations with other AIA filters. These results suggest quasi-steady coronal heating scales with magnetic flux, while rho_tot may contribute to high temperature coronal heating. At lower temperatures, regions with more stored energy than expected for their flux produce less EUV emission than expected.

</details>

Conclusion: 在解释高分辨率SKA-Low太阳射电图像时必须考虑电离层传播效应，尤其是在频段低频端。

Abstract: The Square Kilometre Array Observatory (SKAO) will provide unprecedented angular resolution for low-frequency solar radio imaging, enabling access to fine-scale coronal structures. However, the improved spatial resolution of SKA-Low also increases the sensitivity of solar images to ionospheric propagation effects. We investigate the impact of ionospheric fluctuations on simulated SKA-Low solar observations at 50, 100, 150, and 200 MHz using the \textsc{Karabo} simulator and turbulent, moving ionospheric phase screens. We quantify the resulting image distortions using spatial power spectral density (PSD) analysis and compare the SKA response with corresponding MWA simulations. The results show that ionospheric distortions are strongly scale- and frequency-dependent, with the maximum fractional PSD distortion decreasing with observing frequencies. SKA resolves ionospheric fluctuations over spatial scales that are largely smoothed by the MWA instrumental response, with significant distortions occurring on scales comparable to solar active-region structures. These results demonstrate that ionospheric propagation effects should be considered when interpreting high-resolution SKA-low solar radio images, particularly at the lower end of the frequency band.

</details>

*Saksham Pande,Abhishekh Kumar Srivastava,Fabio Bacchini,Sripan Mondal,Akash Bairagi,Balveer Singh,Kartika Sangal,Sudheer K. Mishra,Xinping Zhou,Ding Yuan,R. -Y. Kwon,B. N. Dwivedi*

TL;DR: 通过2.5D电阻MHD模拟，研究了导向场对日冕电流片形成与演化的影响，发现强导向场会延迟并抑制重联，重联率与电流片长宽比呈现随导向场变化的分段幂律演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日冕电流片中的磁重联是太阳日冕的基本释能机制，但导向场在其形成与自洽演化中的作用尚未被充分理解，因此需要系统研究导向场如何调控局部重联电流片的形成与演化。

Method: 采用2.5D电阻磁流体力学（MHD）数值模拟，设置不同强度的导向场，考察局部重联日冕电流片的形成与演化过程，并分析重联率、电流片长宽比、最高温度与出流速度等指标的幂律行为。

Result: 强导向场会阻止反向磁力线充分接近，从而难以形成电流片并延迟、抑制其后续演化。重联率上升阶段近似幂律，指数δ1随导向场增强而减小；电流片长宽比呈类似幂律趋势，指数α1随导向场增强而减小。后续较慢增长阶段中，δ2与α2均随导向场增强而增大；该阶段源于电流片内导向场在入流无法被有效抵抗后出现急剧下降。重联阶段中最高温度和出流速度的幂律指数也随导向场增强而减小。

Conclusion: 自洽形成的电流片在非零导向场作用下会经历重联机制被高效与低效淬灭的不同阶段。研究量化了重联过程各指标的行为，并讨论了其对观测表观电流片及探测背景日冕条件的适用性。

Abstract: Although magnetic reconnection in coronal current sheets (CSs) is a fundamental energy release mechanism in the solar corona, the role of guide-fields in governing its formation and self-consistent evolution is not fully understood. Through 2.5D resistive magnetohydrodynamic (MHD) simulations, we investigate how guide-fields affect the formation and evolution of a localized reconnecting coronal CS. Strong guide-fields prevent oppositely directed field lines from approaching sufficiently to form a CS, delaying and suppressing its subsequent evolution. The reconnection rate exhibits a rising phase resembling a power-law, with exponent $δ_1$ decreasing with rising guide-fields, indicating slower temporal growth of the reconnection rate. The aspect ratio of the CS follows a similar power-law trend with the exponent $α_1$ decreasing with rising guide-fields. A subsequent slower growth phase of the reconnection rate shows a power-law exponent $δ_2$ that increases with rising guide-fields, as does the corresponding aspect-ratio exponent $α_2$. As the CS evolves, the latter phase is achieved due to a sharp decremental transition of the guide-field within the CS after a time when the increasing inflows are inefficiently resisted by the guide-field itself. In the reconnection regime during prior phases, the power-law exponents of the maximum temperature and the outflow velocities also decrease with rising guide-fields demonstrating that stronger guide-fields suppress the growth of these indicators also. This study suggests that a self-consistently formed CS displays phases of efficient and inefficient quenching of the reconnection mechanism, promoted by non-zero guide-fields. We also quantify this behavior for indicators of the reconnection process and discuss their applicability to observations of apparent CSs and probing background coronal conditions.

</details>

Abstract: Aims. We have mapped the dust and molecular gas emission distributions in the vicinity (a few stellar radii) of the asymptotic giant branch (AGB) star EP\,Aquarii, with a previously reported stellar companion at $\sim$50\,au, to search for binary-induced asymmetries. Methods. We present an analysis of new high-angular-resolution ($\gtrsim$4\,mas) images obtained with MATISSE (in the wavelength range $\sim$3--8\,\mum) as well as archival high-angular-resolution ($\gtrsim$20\,mas) ALMA $\rm^{12}$CO\,(c$J$=2-1) line and continuum emission observations ($\sim$230\,GHz), to study the circumstellar morphology and kinematics of the molecular gas on these spatial scales. Results. The MATISSE images display a bright central source and two slightly extended emission features ("blobs") located on opposite sides of the AGB star (at radial distances of $\sim$4--10\,au). These structures are consistent with previous mid-infrared interferometric observations, suggesting stability over decades. The central source is elongated along the direction defined by the blobs, in both the MATISSE and ALMA continuum images. The ALMA $\rm^{12}$CO\,($J$=2-1) maps display an asymmetric inner wind with a velocity gradient. Conclusions. The observations are consistent with an edge-on dusty equatorial overdensity (disc-like), hinting the presence of a close ($\lesssim$4\,au) companion. The inferred symmetry axis is almost perpendicular to the CO velocity gradient and the orbital plane proposed for the distant ($\sim$50\,au) companion. EP\,Aqr may represent the first misaligned hierarchical triple system with a central AGB star.

Coleman,Georgina Dransfield,Yilen Gómez Maqueo Chew,Leslie Hebb,Neda Heidari,Vedad Kunovac,James McCormac,Nikki J. Miller,Richard Nelson,Martin Nielsen,Alexandre Santerne,Madison G. TL;DR: EBLM-North项目对50个低质量食双星进行均匀研究，测得的恒星半径和质量精度分别为4.2%和8.6%，结果与Baraffe和MIST模型统计一致，未发现显著的半径膨胀或金属度趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确测量宿主恒星对寻找类地系外行星至关重要，M型矮星是理想目标。低质量食双星为经验性测定低质量恒星的半径和质量提供了独特机会，因为恒星模型在这些区域可能存在系统偏差。

Method: 结合SOPHIE的高分辨率高精度径向速度与TESS测光数据，构建EBLM-North样本——迄今最大的低质量食双星均匀研究。对50个高质量双星（轨道周期均大于5天）测量次星半径和质量的中位精度，并与Baraffe和MIST等时线模型进行比较。

Result: 次星半径中位精度4.2%，质量精度8.6%。与Baraffe和MIST模型统计一致，约化卡方χ²_ν~1。测得半径膨胀为0.3±1.6%，无统计显著趋势；未探测到与金属度的显著趋势，与MIST预测的金属度趋势不符。χ²_ν~1说明结果散布仅由测量不确定度造成。但次星有效温度未能再现与模型的高一致性，也未恢复预测的金属度趋势。

Conclusion: 观测结果约束了未建模效应对质量-半径关系的影响范围，即不会产生大于4.2%的半径偏差。然而有效温度与模型的吻合度较低，且未重现金属度趋势，提示模型在温度-金属度关系上可能仍有不足。

Abstract: Precise and accurate measurements of host stars are crucial in the search for Earth-like exoplanets, and M-dwarfs are favourable targets in this quest. Low-mass eclipsing binaries offer unique opportunities to empirically determine stellar masses and radii for low-mass stars where stellar models may be systematically biased. We present results from the EBLM-North sample, the largest homogeneous study of low-mass eclipsing binaries with secondary characterisation yet, combining high-resolution and high-precision radial velocities from SOPHIE and TESS photometry. We measure a median precision for stellar companions of 4.2% in radius and 8.6% in mass within a sub-sample of 50 high-quality binaries, all with orbital periods $P_{\rm bin} > 5\,\rm days$. We compare our data to the Baraffe and MIST isochrones, and find our sample is statistically consistent with those models, producing reduced chi-squared values, $χ^2_ν\sim 1$. Our measured radius inflation is $0.3\pm 1.6$% indicating no statistically significant trend. We likewise detect no significant trend with metallicity, despite having the precision to identify such a relationship. As such, our observations do not appear to follow the general metallicity trend predicted in MIST. A $χ_ν^2 \sim 1$ indicates the scatter in our results is commensurate with our uncertainties alone. These results constrain the scope for unmodelled effects on the mass-radius relation that would produce radius deviations greater than 4.2%. However, our secondary effective temperatures do not reproduce this high level of agreement with the models, and we do not recover the predicted trends with metallicity.

</details>

Conclusion: 经适当校准后，可确保使用完整的EBLM SB1样本来约束银河系中最小却最丰富恒星的质量-半径-光度关系时的准确性。

Abstract: Eclipsing binaries consisting of a solar-type primary and an M dwarf secondary are key laboratories to study objects at the bottom of the main sequence. Fundamental parameters of such stars have a direct impact upon exoplanetary studies, as searches for temperate Earth-sized planets focus on M dwarf hosts. Eclipsing binaries are essential to measure these parameters. However for stellar masses $<$ 0.35 M$_\odot$ , few double-lined eclipsing systems are known. Thus, stellar evolution models and empirical relations have had to rely on single-lined eclipsing binaries, for instance those from the EBLM project (Eclipsing Binary - Low Mass). Using high resolution spectroscopy to extract the weak signal of the secondary (flux ratio $<$ 1 per cent in the optical), we transform five more systems from single to double-lined, deriving absolute masses for both stars. We then compare methods typically employed to estimate stellar masses for field stars or single-lined binaries with the absolute dynamical masses we obtain, to assess their accuracy. Our results for SB1 and SB2 analyses are mostly consistent within 1-$σ$, though we identify a small systematic overestimation of SB1 masses derived from a combination of radial velocities and eclipse photometry. We show that the bias originates from primary radius overestimation during isochronal analysis, wherein the secondary light reduces photometric magnitudes and impacts the recovered radii by modifying the spectral energy distribution. With proper calibration, we can ensure the accuracy of using the full EBLM SB1 sample to constrain mass-radius-luminosity relationships for the smallest, yet most abundant, stars in the Galaxy

</details>

*Andrew R. Casey,Megan Bedell,Adam Wheeler,Julianne Dalcanton,Lily Zhao*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究将恒星元素丰度与凝聚温度的趋势作为岩石行星形成的示踪物，但这一解释可能受到其他物理效应的干扰，作者希望提出并验证替代性解释。

Result: 发现恒星活动指标log R'_HK与凝聚温度斜率相关；多种星斑配置可模拟出随凝聚温度变化的丰度模式；极端情况下高凝聚温度元素丰度可增加约三倍（0.5 dex）。

Abstract: Trends with stellar elemental abundances and condensation temperature have been proposed as a possible tracer of rocky planet formation. Here we present an alternative explanation, where we show that stellar activity (star spots) can also cause apparent abundance changes in refractory elements preferentially over volatile elements. This effect arises naturally from atomic physics: refractory (rocky; high-$T_c$) elements preferentially have low first-ionization potentials, with strong transitions that are most sensitive to the cooler, magnetically-perturbed spot conditions, making any spot-induced change in line strength correlated with $T_c$. We re-examine literature on Sun-like stars in the Solar neighborhood and find that the $\log{R'_{HK}}$ stellar activity indicator is correlated with the so-called $T_c$ slope: the gradient of chemical abundances with respect to condensation temperature. Using 1D LTE Korg.jl simulations of the Sun with spots of varying size and contrast, we show that a wide range of spot configurations can manifest as abundance patterns that mimic a signal of abundance trends with condensation temperature. Finally, we conduct a line-by-line differential analysis of an ambient Solar spectrum relative to a Solar sunspot umbra to represent an extreme case, where we find that elements with high condensation temperature can show a factor of three (0.5 dex) increase. We discuss implications that stellar activity poses for precision spectroscopy, and provide some direction on how this effect might be mitigated.

</details>


### [73] [Anchoring the Chromospheric Activity$-$Rotation Relation: A Study of the Pleiades and Group X](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18644)

Motivation: 探究低质量恒星自转与色球Hα活动的演化规律，特别是自转-活动关系何时达到成熟形式，以及色球演化是否仅由自转周期单一因素决定。

For F through mid K stars, the Group X color$-$period sequence lies between those of the Pleiades and of Praesepe, as expected. But the $P_\mathrm{rot}$ for its late K and early M dwarfs and the Praesepe slow-rotator sequence overlap, indicating that the 700 Myr-old stars have not spun down significantly despite being nearly twice as old as their Group X counterparts. This implies that the chromospheric activity$-$rotation relation attains its mature form between 120 and 400 Myr. Dividing the sample by color reveals that K, M0, and M1 dwarfs are no longer saturated by $\approx$120 Myr, M2 and M3 dwarfs become unsaturated between $\approx$400 and $\approx$700 Myr, and later M dwarfs remain largely saturated through field ages even as their saturated $L_\mathrm{Hα}/L_\mathrm{bol}$ levels decline, indicating that chromospheric evolution is not determined by rotation period alone.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望明确巴尔末系各条谱线的光度响应是否存在随谱线级数（上能级量子数）的系统性变化，以检验不同谱线在光度定标中的可用性。

Abstract: We investigate the differential luminosity response of the optical Balmer series from H8 to H-alpha in Galactic B5-A5 luminous stars using homogeneous measurements of central depth, FWHM, and equivalent width at a resolving power of approximately 12,000. Direct line-to-line comparisons show a systematic change in the central-depth response toward the lower-order transitions. For the primary 72-star luminosity sample, the fitted luminosity coefficients show an overall positive trend with upper-level quantum number, with a point-estimate gradient of dq/dn = 0.0419. A joint Monte Carlo analysis with 10,000 realisations gives a median dq/dn = 0.0387 and a 95 percent interval from 0.0253 to 0.0532, with no non-positive gradients obtained. The higher-order H8-H-delta coefficients form a statistically unresolved plateau, followed by a well-supported increase in response magnitude from H-delta toward H-alpha. The result is robust to the adopted effective-temperature scale. The FWHM coefficients show no comparable Balmer-order progression, and the directly fitted profile-shape response is consistent with zero from H8 through H-beta. For H-alpha, the full logarithmic sample is not formally significant, whereas removal of the three remaining strongly filled-core profiles yields a significantly negative shape response. The observed behaviour is qualitatively compatible with the systematic variation of pressure broadening across the Balmer series, while the enhanced H-alpha response may additionally reflect non-LTE and wind-related effects.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 原位球状星团的均匀年龄与AMR支持以并合事件解释年龄分布结构与部分异常星团，并给出宇宙年龄及哈勃常数约束；系统误差主要来自恒星模型给出绝对年龄零点的不确定性。

Abstract: Within the homogeneous framework for the derivation of GC ages defined by the CARMA collaboration, we focus on the clusters born in-situ in the MW. As they are the oldest among the old GCs, their absolute age provides constraints about the age of the Universe. Moreover, the shape of their AMR is expected to show the imprints of past merger events, in case these were able to trigger GC formation in the Galaxy. We derive new homogeneous ages for 18 GCs dynamically and/or chemically classified as in-situ, and build the MW AMR from a total sample of 34 GCs. We find that all the selected GCs follow a well defined AMR, with the exception of NGC6642 and Pal1. While the former is likely a member of the LKH merger event, thus putting chronological evidence for the existence of this progenitor on even more robust ground, for the latter we propose an alternative scenario according to which it formed in-situ, but in a metal-depleted region of the Galaxy, possibly due to the interaction with an unspecified merger event. To find evidence of merger-driven GC formation, we use a GMM for the observed age distribution. This is statistically best-fit by 4 components, which we associate with significant events of the MW merger history. Cluster ages were finally calibrated to a more accurate absolute age scale based on asteroseismic measurements of the mass of red giant stars in NGC6791, and the resulting AMR was fit to compute the look-back time for the beginning of cluster formation in the MW. The fit yields t_GC,i=13.59+0.33-0.32 Gyr for initial cluster formation, and tU=13.79+/-0.34 Gyr for the age of the Universe. This value translates to H0=68.72+3.95-3.49(stat.)+2.65-2.32(sys.) km s-1 Mpc-1, where the systematic error comes from a realistic estimate of the zero point uncertainty on the GC absolute ages derived from stellar models.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 银河系中铁储库的分布是理解星际介质中尘埃颗粒生长机制的关键。高分辨率X射线吸收谱可通过近吸收边处的X射线吸收精细结构（XAFS）探测含铁尘埃的化学状态，而XRISM/Resolve首次在Fe K-edge（7.1 keV）提供足够谱分辨率，使在高银道柱密度视线上研究尘埃化学成为可能。

Method: 对XRISM性能验证目标GX 340+0（持续明亮的低质量X射线双星，视线氢柱密度6.6×10²² cm⁻²，Fe K-edge处光学深度充足）进行Fe K-edge的XAFS分析，通过联合拟合尘埃与气体模型推断铁的耗竭程度及各固相组分比例。

Result: 沿视线方向Fe的尘埃耗竭率为95%；固相铁中约38%为金属铁、47%为铁硫化物，其余为硅酸盐。金属铁与铁硫化物的高占比与以往弥漫星际介质的X射线研究结果明显不同。

Abstract: Understanding the reservoirs of iron (Fe) in our Galaxy is crucial for probing the mechanisms that drive dust grain growth in the interstellar medium (ISM). High-resolution X-ray absorption spectroscopy offers a powerful tool to investigate the chemistry of dust grains, particularly those containing Fe, through spectroscopic features near photoabsorption edges known as X-ray Absorption Fine Structures (XAFS). With the Resolve spectrometer aboard XRISM, we can now examine the Fe K-edge (7.1 keV) owing to its improved spectral resolution in this energy range. This enabled us to probe the dust grain chemistry in high Galactic column densities. In this work, we present a pilot study on dust grain chemistry through the Fe K-edge of the XRISM Performance Verification target GX 340+0. This source is a persistently bright low-mass X-ray binary with a hydrogen column density of 6.6x10^22 cm^-2 along the line of sight, providing sufficient optical depth at the Fe K-edge. By fitting combined dust and gas models, we find that Fe is depleted by 95% into dust along the line of sight towards the source. Moreover, we find evidence that 38% of the solid-phase Fe is in metallic iron, 47% in iron sulfides, and the remainder in silicates. The large contribution from metallic iron and iron sulfides differs from what is typically inferred from X-ray studies of diffuse ISM and may suggest that that these compounds become more significant reservoirs of Fe in higher column density Galactic sightlines.

Abstract: We present a systematic interferometric search for close supermassive black hole (SMBH) binaries in 20 nearby type 1 active galactic nuclei (AGN) observed with VLTI/\grav. We investigate parsec-scale asymmetric emission by modeling the squared visibilities and closure phases with a single circular Gaussian component plus a background point source for the central AGN, and searching for additional compact companions through grid-based binary models. The robustness of the search is validated using injection-recovery simulations and point-spread-function diagnostics. Several features are identified in the initial significance maps, but only one candidate out of 20 shows a persistent compact asymmetry, corresponding to an observed incidence of $5\%$. If NGC~3783 is interpreted as a binary AGN, we derive a Poissonian one-sided $1σ$ upper limit of $f_{\rm bin}<16.5\%$ for GRAVITY-detectable binaries among bright nearby AGN (K<12.5) at separations of $\sim1$-$100$~pc. Conversely, if the observed asymmetry in NGC~3783 is associated with asymmetric circumnuclear dust emission, the corresponding upper limit on the binary fraction is $f_{\rm bin}<9.2\%$. We compare these results with predictions from the Horizon-AGN simulation, finding comparable binary fractions. We also noted that the expected fraction strongly depends on the assumed distribution of individual SMBH accretion rates within binaries, which is currently unresolved in cosmological models. Our results demonstrate the potential of infrared interferometry to identify parsec-scale binary AGN candidates and motivate future GRAVITY+ observations to expand the sample and follow up known candidates, helping to confirm their nature and distinguish genuine binaries from asymmetric nuclear structures.

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 通过N体模拟研究了中等质量黑洞（IMBH）扰动下银心恒星盘与核星团的动力学演化，揭示了盘分裂、S星形成及IMBH动力学特征。

Motivation: 探究银河系中心年轻恒星盘的多组分结构及S星起源，检验IMBH对恒星盘扰动和S星形成的作用机制。

Conclusion: IMBH的独特特征为分离良好的盘组分和局限于其轨道扫过区的偏心率激发；最偏心S星可能需Hills机制等额外通道形成。研究还量化了IMBH对恒星损耗锥供给、潮汐瓦解率和双星分离率的影响。

Abstract: We present direct $N$-body simulations of a stellar disc around the supermassive black hole Sgr~A*, perturbed by an intermediate-mass black hole (IMBH) and embedded in a live nuclear star cluster. A retrograde IMBH with $m_\bullet=2000\,M_\odot$, relative inclination $θ_\bullet=150^\circ$, and initial semi-major axis $a_\bullet=0.15$~pc breaks the disc into three disc-like components and an angular momentum filament. The stochastic torques of the cluster, together with the IMBH's inward migration, increase the angular separation between these substructures. By ${\sim}5$~Myr, their separations become comparable to those among the observed Galactic-centre young star structures. The innermost ($a\le0.04$~pc) disc stars evolve into an S-star-like population. Relaxation driven by the cluster disperses their orbital planes, excites their eccentricities and depletes the `zone of avoidance' within ${\sim}10$~Myr even without an IMBH. The IMBH further accelerates their orbital-plane reorientation. However, the most eccentric S-stars may require an additional formation channel, such as the Hills mechanism. The distinctive signatures of the IMBH are the well-separated disc components and the eccentricity excitation confined to the radial range swept by its orbit. Dynamical friction from the cluster reduces the orbital size of a $2000\,M_\odot$ IMBH initially at $0.15$~pc by more than a factor of two within $10$~Myr. Prograde IMBHs tend to align with the stellar disc, whereas a $2000\,M_\odot$ retrograde IMBH can anti-align with the radially overlapping disc stars. We also quantify how the IMBH affects the supply of stars to the loss cone and the rates of stellar tidal disruptions and binary separations.

</details>


### [79] [Globular cluster Observations for Luminosity-dispersion Distances (GOLD): Project description and velocity catalogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14181)
*K. Fahrion,M. A. Beasley,A. Gvozdenko,I. Ortega-Casas,A. Ferre-Mateu,F. I. Aros,D. Dold,T. Jerabkova,S. S. Larsen,O. Müller,M. Rejkuba,K. L. Rhode,G. van de Ven*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文介绍GOLD项目，利用VLT/FLAMES观测19个星系中的球状星团，基于M_V-σ关系测定独立距离，并公开了1062个球状星团和101个超紧凑矮星系的视向速度星表。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要一种不依赖传统距离阶梯、可独立测定星系距离的方法。利用银河系球状星团（GC）标定的绝对星等-速度弥散（M_V-σ）关系，可以为早型和晚型星系提供独立距离，从而检验和补充现有距离测量。

Method: 使用ESO甚大望远镜（VLT）的FLAMES/GIRAFFE对19个早型和晚型星系中的球状星团进行光谱观测。光谱覆盖8483-9000 Å，包含钙三重线区域，分辨率R~18000，视向速度不确定度为1-2 km/s。主要利用速度区分河外星源与前景区分星，并辅以测光和尺寸测量。

Result: 目前已观测17个星系，给出包含1062个球状星团和101个超紧凑矮星系（UCD）的初始样本，其中超过650个天体为首次速度测量。速度与已有研究吻合良好，星表已公开。测光和尺寸数据也已提供。

Conclusion: 除主目标（精确距离测定）外，该数据还可用于研究宿主星系晕动力学与聚类分析、GC和UCD的质光比、恒星初始质量函数、GC的黑洞占据分数，以及从高色散光谱中提取化学丰度。

Abstract: We introduce and describe the Globular cluster Observations for Luminosity-Dispersion distances (GOLD) project, which has the principal aim of determining independent distances based on the globular cluster (GC) absolute magnitude-velocity dispersion ($M_V - σ$) relation calibrated with Milky Way GCs with Gaia distances. The project comprises observations of GCs in 19 early- and late-type galaxies taken with FLAMES/GIRAFFE on ESO's Very Large Telescope. So far, 17 galaxies have been observed. The spectra provide line-of-sight velocities with typical uncertainties of $Δv = 1-2$ km s$^{-1}$. Using primarily velocities to separate extragalactic sources from foreground stars, we present here our initial sample of 1062 GCs and 101 ultra-compact dwarf galaxies (UCDs). For more than 650 of these objects we present the first velocity measurement. We discuss the available photometry and size measurements, and show that our velocities are in good agreement with previous studies. Our velocity catalogues are made publicly available. Beyond the principal aim of obtaining precision distances, additional science to be explored with these data includes host galaxy halo dynamics and clustering analysis, mass-to light ratios of GCs and UCDs in the sample, the stellar initial mass function and black hole occupation fraction of the GCs, and chemical abundances from high-dispersion spectra.

### [80] [Beyond the Damped Random Walk: Multiband AGN Variability from SDSS to Rubin LSST](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14618)
*Izak Schmidlkofer,Zhirui Hu,Weixiang Yu,Hyungsuk Tak*

TL;DR: 本文用多变量连续时间自回归移动平均（MCARMA）模型超越DRW刻画AGN光变，在SDSS Stripe 82样本中134个AGN有109个（81.3%）被选中更高阶模型，并模拟评估LSST条件下高阶结构的可恢复性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AGN光变常用阻尼随机游走（DRW）建模，但日益丰富的多波段时域巡天需要更灵活的随机模型，以刻画超出单一特征时标的变异性并利用波段间依赖关系。

Result: 134个AGN中109个（81.3%）支持更高阶模型，多数为实质性支持，收敛诊断表明该比例偏保守；模型显示强跨波段依赖，且随波长间隔增大而减弱。LSST模拟中，模型阶数恢复强烈依赖内在随机行为、源亮度与观测节奏；许多设置下恢复率高，但当高阶特征难以与多变量DRW区分时恢复率下降，且该不可区分性可在多种内在变异性下持续。

Conclusion: 所选SDSS样本中普遍存在超出多变量DRW的随机结构，其能否被LSST恢复取决于观测条件与内在随机变异形式；方法实现于Python包mcarma。

Abstract: The flux variability of Active Galactic Nuclei (AGNs) is commonly modeled using the damped random walk (DRW), but increasingly rich multiband time-domain surveys motivate more flexible stochastic models that characterize variability beyond a single timescale and exploit dependence across bands. Multivariate continuous-time autoregressive moving average (MCARMA) processes extend the multivariate DRW to higher-order stochastic dynamics and provide a matrix-valued power spectral density for characterizing marginal variability and cross-band dependence. In a carefully selected Sloan Digital Sky Survey (SDSS) Stripe~82 sample, higher-order models are selected for 109 of 134 AGNs (81.3\%), with substantial rather than marginal support in most objects; convergence diagnostics indicate that this fraction is conservative. The fitted models also reveal strong cross-band dependence that generally decreases with wavelength separation. Motivated by this richer structure, we investigate its recoverability under Vera C. Rubin Observatory Legacy Survey of Space and Time (LSST) observing conditions. Model-order recovery depends strongly on the underlying stochastic behavior as well as source brightness and observing cadence. Recovery is high across many of the simulated settings, but deteriorates when the characteristic features of higher-order variability become difficult to distinguish from the multivariate DRW. Additional simulations show that this loss of distinguishability can persist over a range of underlying variability behaviors. These results show that stochastic structure beyond the multivariate DRW is prevalent in our selected SDSS sample, while its recoverability with LSST depends on both observational conditions and the form of the underlying stochastic variability. The methodology is implemented in the Python package mcarma.

Conclusion: 光学激发诊断能可靠定位AGN激发气体，但在最遮蔽的(U)LIRGs中会系统性低估AGN向宿主星系供应的光度。

Abstract: We present resolved optical integral-field spectroscopy of the 32 luminous and ultraluminous infrared galaxy systems of the Multiphase Analysis of (U)LIRG Nuclear Activity (MAUNA) sample, drawn from the GOALS survey and selected to have JWST/MIRI medium-resolution spectrometer (MRS) observations. The data set combines new Keck/KCWI observations of 10 systems with archival VLT/MUSE cubes for 22 systems, covering H$β$ to [S II] at ~1 arcsecond spatial resolution (~0.9 kpc at the sample median distance). Using the optical [N II]/H$α$ BPT diagram, we find that composite and LINER-like excitation together account for most of the classified area (median fractions 0.44 and 0.26), while Seyfert-like gas covers a median fraction of 0.02 but carries a far larger proportion of the line luminosity where it is present. Both the projected size of the Seyfert-classified region, $R_{\rm Sy}=0.08$-$7.5$ kpc, and its [O III] luminosity increase with the bolometric AGN luminosity, with $R_{\rm Sy}\propto L_{\rm AGN,bol}^{0.54\pm0.18}$, as expected for gas photoionized at a constant ionization parameter. The scatter about the luminosity relation depends on dust opacity: the [O III] luminosity per unit AGN luminosity falls by $1.09\pm0.25$ dex per unit depth of the 9.7 $μ$m silicate absorption feature (Spearman $ρ=-0.60$, $p=0.001$). Optical excitation diagnostics therefore locate AGN-excited gas reliably but systematically underestimate the luminosity the AGN supplies to the host in the most obscured (U)LIRGs.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化学族群的空间结构约束着银河系盘的演化及其物理过程。Gaia XP 光谱提供了目前规模最大的光谱样本之一，因此可用来系统研究银河系盘单一丰度族群（MAPs）的空间分布，检验盘结构的简单模型。

Method: 构建红巨星支（RGB）样本，并按 [α/M] 与 [M/H] 划分为 MAPs。样本覆盖以太阳为中心、6×6 kpc 的银盘区域，延伸至 |Z|<3.7 kpc。利用 Gaia 测光母样本估计 XP 完备度，结合 PARSEC 等时线与 Kroupa 初始质量函数计算选择函数，把观测恒星计数转换为本征恒星质量密度。对垂向密度剖面用指数模型拟合得到标高，并对拟合剖面积分得到面质量密度。

Result: 所得剖面总体上与 APOGEE 结果一致。所有 MAPs 都显示 flaring：高-α 族群 flaring 最强，贫金属和亚太阳低-α 族群最弱。各 MAPs 的面质量密度剖面差异显著：高-α 与富金属低-α 族群近似指数衰减，而其他低-α 族群呈现平台状特征，说明银盘不能用简单单指数剖面描述。

Conclusion: 这些差异性结构表明银河系盘具有复杂的组装历史，可能由诞生条件、长期动力学加热、径向迁移以及外部扰动共同调控。

Abstract: The spatial structures of chemical populations constrain Galactic disk evolution and its underlying physical processes. Gaia XP spectra provide one of the largest spectroscopic samples available. We use Gaia XP spectra to study Galactic disk mono-abundance populations (MAPs), measuring their scale heights as a function of radius and radial surface-mass-density profiles. We construct a red giant branch (RGB) sample and divide it into MAPs according to $[α/{\rm M}]$ and $[{\rm M/H}]$. The sample covers a $6\times6$ kpc region centered on the Sun in the Galactic plane and extends to $|Z|<3.7$ kpc. We estimate XP completeness using a Gaia photometric parent sample and combine PARSEC isochrones with a Kroupa initial mass function (IMF) to calculate the selection function and convert observed stellar counts into intrinsic stellar mass densities. We fit vertical density profiles with an exponential model to derive scale heights and integrate the fitted profiles to obtain surface mass densities. The resulting profiles are generally consistent with those from APOGEE. All MAPs show flaring, with the high-$α$ population exhibiting the strongest flaring and the metal-poor and sub-solar low-$α$ populations showing the weakest. Surface-mass-density profiles differ significantly among MAPs: the high-$α$ and metal-rich low-$α$ populations show approximately exponential declines, whereas the other low-$α$ populations exhibit plateau-like features, indicating that the Galactic disk cannot be described by a simple single-exponential profile. These differential structures suggest a complex Galactic disk assembly history likely regulated by birth conditions, secular dynamical heating, radial migration, and external perturbations.

</details>


### [83] [Photometric Identification and Dynamical Purification of Blue Horizontal Branch Stars in DESI MWS DR1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15031)
*Shuai Zhang,Xiaolong Wang,Kai Xiao,Guozhen Hu,Wenyuan Cui,Ce Cai,Shuai Feng,Jie Ju*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文基于DESI MWS DR1光谱合成SkyMapper测光并利用HDBSCAN在颜色空间识别BHB候选体，通过KDE贝叶斯模型与逃逸速度筛选，构建高纯度的测光BHB样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蓝水平分支（BHB）恒星是描绘银河系星晕结构的理想示踪体，但传统光谱识别受限于数据质量和样本规模，宽波段测光方法在完备度和纯度上仅达中等水平，亟需更优的识别方案。

Result: 概率加权纯度达88.9%，完备度94.2%；逃逸速度截断剔除1,461个具有非物理运动学参数的污染体，得到7,776颗恒星的干净样本，保留率84.2%。污染体主要为暗源（$g\sim20$ mag）、信噪比极低（中位SNR<5）。

Conclusion: 逃逸速度筛选本质上是低信噪比导致距离膨胀的物理探测器，误用BHB颜色-光度关系会放大空间速度至超出银河系逃逸极限，从而有效识别并剔除低质量污染源。

Abstract: Blue horizontal-branch (BHB) stars are ideal tracers for mapping the structure of the Galactic stellar halo. However, traditional spectroscopic identification is limited by data quality and sample size, while broad-band photometric methods often achieve only moderate completeness and purity. Building upon the color-space BHB identification method of \citet{2025ApJS..278...62H}, we synthesize SkyMapper $(u,v,g,z)$ photometry from DESI MWS DR1 spectroscopy and identify 9,237 BHB candidates in the $(g-z)_0$--$(u-v)_0$ color space using the HDBSCAN clustering algorithm. We then construct a Kernel Density Estimation (KDE) Bayesian probability model with capture-recapture (C-R) prior to assign posterior membership probabilities, achieving a probability-weighted purity of 88.9\% and completeness of 94.2\%. Applying an escape-velocity cut based on the Galactic gravitational potential, we remove 1,461 contaminants with unphysical kinematic parameters, yielding a clean photometric BHB sample $\mathcal{S}_c$ of 7,776 stars (retention rate $R_{\rm keep} = 84.2\%$). Systematic analysis of the contaminants reveals that they are predominantly faint sources ($g\sim20$~mag) with very low signal-to-noise ratios (median SNR~$<$~5). When the BHB color--luminosity relation is incorrectly applied to these objects, their space velocities are amplified beyond the Galactic escape limit, confirming that the escape-velocity filter essentially acts as a physical detector of low-SNR--driven distance inflation.

### [84] [The NIVARIA-Local Group simulations: Unveiling the origin of rotation curve diversity in dwarf galaxies and its connection to galaxy morphology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15071)
*A. Contreras-Santos,A. Di Cintio,A. Negri,J. Sarrato-Alós,S. Cardona-Barrero,G. García-Bethencourt,C. Brook,N. I. Libeskind,A. Knebe,A. Macciò*

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矮星系旋转曲线形状存在巨大差异，对ΛCDM范式下的星系形成模型构成挑战，需要真实局域群环境下的模拟来理解这一多样性。

Method: 结合HESTIA约束的局域群初始条件与NIHAO星系形成模型，运行中等分辨率宇宙学模拟，分析质量在10^7至10^10太阳质量之间的孤立矮星系的结构与运动学性质。

Abstract: Dwarf galaxies exhibit a large diversity in rotation curve shapes, challenging galaxy formation models within the $Λ$CDM paradigm. We present NIVARIA-LG, a new suite of cosmological simulations studying dwarf galaxy evolution in realistic Local Group environments. This intermediate-resolution pilot lays the groundwork for the full-scale NIVARIA project. The simulations combine constrained Local Group initial conditions from HESTIA with the NIHAO galaxy formation model. The simulated galaxies reproduce observed stellar-to-halo mass relations, match the Baryonic Tully-Fisher relation, and capture the distribution of stellar effective radii, including compact and diffuse galaxies. We analyse the structural and kinematic properties of isolated dwarf galaxies with $10^7 \lesssim M_\star/M_\odot \lesssim 10^{10}$, focusing on rotation curve diversity. NIVARIA-LG naturally produces a broad range of rotation curve shapes, including slowly rising systems underrepresented in previous simulations and steep rotation curves. We find that rotation curve diversity is primarily associated with gas extent and inner dark matter structure: more extended gas and shallower inner density profiles correspond to more slowly rising curves, while stellar effective radius shows little independent association. Individual galaxies show that rotation curve shapes change rapidly as gas is redistributed during assembly. Recent accretion events are also associated with more slowly rising curves in the most massive dwarfs. Together, these results suggest that rotation curve diversity is linked to the baryonic cycle, which shapes the inner dark matter distribution. The Local Group environment and its filaments influence this evolution by regulating gas accretion and redistribution, producing the observed diversity in rotation curve shapes.
