### [85] [Anisotropic Radiation in Super-Eddington Active Galactic Nuclei: The Responsivity of Broad-Line Regions to Continuum Variability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15255)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超爱丁顿吸积AGN理论上应产生各向异性电离辐射，而以往反响映射观测到超爱丁顿AGN中Hβ相对连续谱的时延系统性缩短，暗示这一各向异性。作者希望进一步通过Hβ响应度系统检验该各向异性，并与亚爱丁顿AGN对比。

Method: 对亚爱丁顿与超爱丁顿AGN系统测量Hβ谱线响应度（Hβ变化幅度与驱动连续谱变化幅度之比）；结合经连续谱-发射线时延修正后的有效响应度与光致电离计算，推断BLR实际接收的电离辐射场。

Result: 超爱丁顿AGN中，由Hβ响应度推断的电离通量与沿视线方向观测的通量存在显著差异；而亚爱丁顿AGN中两者较为一致，为超爱丁顿AGN电离辐射的强各向异性提供了有力证据。

Conclusion: 结果支持超爱丁顿AGN中存在强各向异性电离辐射，并讨论了该各向异性对JWST新发现的"小红点"天体的潜在意义。

Abstract: Anisotropic ionizing radiation is a natural theoretical expectation for super-Eddington accretion in active galactic nuclei (AGNs), implying that the ionizing flux incident on the broad-line region (BLR) clouds differs from that measured along the observer's line of sight. Reverberation mapping studies have revealed systematically shortened time lags of the broad H$β$ emission line relative to continuum variations in super-Eddington AGNs, a phenomenon that can be naturally interpreted as a consequence of anisotropic ionizing continuum radiation. To further investigate this anisotropy, we perform a systematic analysis of the H$β$ line responsivity, defined as the ratio of the variability amplitude of H$β$ to that of the driving continuum, for both sub- and super-Eddington AGNs. By combining the effective responsivities measured relative to the driving continuum, after correcting for the continuum--emission-line delay, with photoionization calculations, we infer the ionizing radiation field received by their BLRs. We find that, in super-Eddington AGNs, the ionizing flux inferred from the H$β$ responsivity differs significantly from that observed along the line of sight, whereas the two are in closer agreement in sub-Eddington AGNs. These results provide compelling evidence for strong anisotropy of the incident ionizing radiation in super-Eddington AGNs. We conclude by discussing the broader implications of this anisotropy, including its potential relevance to the "little red dots" recently discovered by the James Webb Space Telescope.

</details>


### [86] [Modelling the broken age-[Fe/H] relation and metallicity distribution function of mono-age populations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15295)
*Qinhao Shao,Jianhui Lian*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年观测显示太阳邻域恒星年龄-金属丰度关系存在明显断裂，暗示银河系恒星族群可能分为两条演化序列；同时恒星径向迁移对银河系化学结构的影响程度尚不明确，需要定量约束。

Method: 基于银河系化学演化模型解释年龄-金属丰度关系断裂；在多区化学演化模型中引入简化的径向迁移处理，将其建模为高斯分布刻画的概率扩散过程；通过拟合单年龄恒星族群的金属丰度分布函数（MDF）与观测对比，推断迁移强度。

Result: 对位于9.5-11.5 kpc、年龄2-6 Gyr的恒星，推断迁移强度为 σ = 2.15^{+0.100}_{-0.103} kpc，对应10 Gyr内平均径向位移1.92 kpc，该结果低于此前对恒星径向迁移的估计。

Conclusion: 年龄-金属丰度关系断裂可由近期主气体吸积事件（稀释盘金属丰度并触发星暴，形成独立演化序列）解释；径向迁移在塑造银河系化学结构中的作用比以往估计的更不重要。

Abstract: This study investigates the chemical evolution history and radial migration in the Milky Way utilizing high-precision age measurements based on large-scale stellar spectroscopic surveys. Recent observational data have revealed a distinct break in the age-metallicity relation of stars in the solar neighborhood, suggesting that stellar populations of the Milky Way can be divided into two evolutionary sequences. Based on galactic chemical evolution models, we confirm that this break in the age-metallicity relation can be explained by a recent major gas accretion event, which diluted the metallicity in the disk and triggered a starburst, leading to the formation of an independent age-metallicity sequence. To assess the impact of stellar radial migration, we incorporate a simplified treatment of this process into our multi-zone chemical evolution model. Radial migration is modeled as a probabilistic diffusion process, characterized by a Gaussian distribution. By fitting the model-predicted metallicity distribution functions (MDFs) of mono-age stellar populations to observations, we infer a migration strength of $σ= 2.15^{+0.100}_{-0.103}\,{\rm kpc}$ for stars with ages between 2 and 6 Gyr located at 9.5-11.5 kpc, corresponding to an average radial displacement of 1.92 kpc over 10 Gyr. This result is lower than previous estimates of stellar radial migration, suggesting that the role of radial migration in shaping the chemical structure of the Milky Way is less important than previously estimated.

</details>


### [87] [The Tilt of the Velocity Ellipsoid in the Milky Way Disk with GALAH-Gaia Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15349)
*Zhenzhen He,Ruilan Yang,Hui Li,Weixiang Sun*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 利用GALAH和Gaia超过57.2万颗恒星，研究速度椭球倾角对金属丰度的依赖，发现银河系盘星整体趋向柱状对齐，且不同盘族群的α0存在显著金属丰度依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究银河系盘星运动学（速度椭球倾角）是否以及如何依赖金属丰度，并据此推断塑造盘星运动学的主要动力学机制（引力势、棒共振或并合加热等）。

Method: 从GALAH与Gaia选取超过572,000颗恒星，按[α/Fe]划分为低α/Fe和高α/Fe族群，用关系式α=α0·arctan(Z/R)拟合倾角，并进一步拟合α0随[Fe/H]的变化。

Result: α0分别为0.78±0.05（全体）、0.86±0.07（低α/Fe）和0.77±0.07（高α/Fe）。低α/Fe族群α0=0.12 sin(3.72[Fe/H]+1.09)+0.75，高α/Fe族群α0=0.20 sin(2.99[Fe/H]+1.90)+0.71。

Conclusion: 盘族群整体趋向柱状对齐，表明银河系重子盘的引力势在塑造动力学弛豫恒星的运动学中起主导作用；低α/Fe族群的金属丰度依赖可能主要与银河系棒诱导的共振有关，高α/Fe族群还受并合加热、吸积及湍流ISM中形成等额外动力学与演化过程影响。

Abstract: We investigate the metallicity dependence of the tilt of the velocity ellipsoid using more than 572,000 stars selected from GALAH and Gaia. We find that the tilt angles of disk stars can be well described by the relation $α= α_{0}\,\mathrm{arctan}(Z/R)$, and the $α_{0}$ differ significantly among different disk populations, with $α_{0}$ are 0.78 $\pm$ 0.05, 0.86 $\pm$ 0.07, and 0.77 $\pm$ 0.07 for the whole sample, the Low-[$α$/Fe] and the High-[$α$/Fe] populations, respectively. These results indicate that Galactic disk populations are globally tilted toward cylindrical alignment, implying that the gravitational potential of the Galactic baryonic disk plays a dominant role in shaping the kinematics of dynamically relaxed stars. Our detailed analysis further reveals a metallicity dependence of $α_{0}$ for various populations, with the Low-[$α$/Fe] and the High-[$α$/Fe] populations are respectively $α_{0}$ = 0.12 $\mathrm{sin}$(3.72 [Fe/H] + 1.09) + 0.75 and $α_{0}$ = 0.20 $\mathrm{sin}$(2.99 [Fe/H] + 1.90) + 0.71. These results indicate that the metallicity dependence observed in the Low-[$α$/Fe] population may be primarily associated with resonances induced by the Galactic bar, while the behavior of the High-[$α$/Fe] population may also have been affected by additional dynamical and evolutionary processes, such as violent heating associated with mergers, accretion, and/or the formation of these stars in an environment shaped by mergers of gas-rich systems and/or a turbulent ISM.

</details>

*Rashmi Gottumukkala,Gabriel Brammer,Sune Toft,Mengyuan Xiao,Anna de Graaff,Pascal A. Oesch,Francesco Valentino,Katherine E. Whitaker,William M. Baker,Laia Barrufet,Rachel Bezanson,Leindert A. Boogaard,Alex J. Cameron,Olivia R. Cooper,Emma Giovinazzo,Kasper E. Heintz,Michaela Hirschmann,Kei Ito,Ivo Labbe,Minju M. Lee,Zach J. Lewis,Jorryt Matthee,Ian McConachie,Desika Narayanan,Clara L. Pollock,Sedona H. Price,David J. Setton,Marko Shuntov,Pieter van Dokkum,Bingjie Wang,Joris Witstok,Yunchong Zhang,Pengpei Zhu*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本研究基于JWST/NIRSpec PRISM光谱与NIRCam成像，对约12,000个z>2星系进行联合分光光度拟合，发布了一个光谱推导的星系物理性质星表，涵盖恒星质量、恒星形成率、尘埃衰减等，并揭示了近期恒星形成历史与尘埃衰减曲线的变化规律。

Motivation: 刻画宇宙时间内恒星形成、恒星质量与尘埃积累的演化，依赖于对高红移星系恒星族属性的准确表征。现有研究缺乏大样本、光谱推导的高红移星系物理性质星表。

Method: 使用定制的BAGPIPES SED拟合代码，对DAWN JWST档案中JWST/NIRSpec PRISM光谱与NIRCam宽带测光进行联合分光光度建模，样本约12,000个z>2星系。

Conclusion: 该星表凭借空前的样本统计量，独特地支持从宇宙正午到宇宙黎明时期星系恒星形成、淬灭与尘埃性质的研究。

Abstract: Charting the evolution of star-formation, stellar mass and dust build-up over cosmic time relies on the accurate characterisation of the stellar population properties of high redshift galaxies. We present a catalogue of spectroscopically derived physical properties for ~12,000 galaxies at z>2, with archival JWST/NIRSpec PRISM spectroscopy and JWST/NIRCam imaging from the DAWN JWST Archive. We present a custom version of the SED fitting code BAGPIPES, used to perform joint spectrophotometric modelling of PRISM spectra and broadband NIRCam photometry. In doing so, we publicly release a science-ready catalogue of the physical properties of galaxies beyond cosmic noon. We report key physical properties, such as stellar masses, star-formation rates (SFR) and timescales, and V-band dust attenuation (Av). We further probe the recent star-formation histories (SFH) of galaxies via their SFRs on 3 Myr, 10 Myr, and 100 Myr timescales, capturing both rising and falling recent SFHs. Through dust-corrected ugi colour-colour diagrams of massive galaxies, we show how quiescent and dusty galaxies populate this colour space at z>2, with galaxies experiencing a recent downturn in star-formation occupying distinct parameter space. We also study the dustiest galaxies in our sample, showing that they are preferentially more massive and strongly star-forming relative to the parent sample, residing on the high-mass end of the star-forming main-sequence. The slope of the attenuation curve has a strong dependence on Av, varying from steep to shallow as the dust attenuation increases and the specific star-formation rate decreases. Our findings show that the unparalleled number statistics of this catalogue of spectroscopically derived stellar population properties uniquely enables the study star-formation, quenching and dust properties of galaxies from cosmic noon to cosmic dawn.

</details>


### [89] [Constraints on the r-process Delay Time from Dwarf Spheroidal Galaxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15946)
*Lauren E. Henderson,Evan N. Kirby,Mithi A. C. de los Reyes,Rebecca Surman*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 通过一区化学演化模型拟合Sculptor和Draco矮椭球星系中C、Mg、Si、Ca、Fe、Sr、Y、Ba、Eu丰度，约束r过程富集时标，发现中子星并合不能是唯一r过程来源，需要"及时"r过程源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矮椭球星系（dSphs）拥有古老恒星族群和简单结构，是研究早期宇宙化学演化的理想实验室。本文旨在利用其恒星丰度约束r过程的富集时标与来源。

Method: 对Sculptor和Draco两个dSphs建立一区星系化学演化模型，模型中同时包含s过程和r过程，拟合C、Mg、Si、Ca、Fe、Sr、Y、Ba、Eu共9种元素的丰度。

Result: 中子星并合不能是Sculptor和Draco中r过程的唯一来源；若仅以中子星并合为r过程来源，其最小延迟时间须仅为14-16 Myr。若采用较长延迟时间并搭配与大质量恒星演化同时标的"及时"r过程源（如磁旋转超新星），也能拟合中子俘获元素丰度。Sr和Y丰度对每次r过程事件的产额给出了限制，理论r过程产额高估Sr和Y产量最多达100倍。

Conclusion: Sculptor和Draco的观测丰度要求存在"及时"r过程源；中子星并合单独无法解释观测，需与其他快速r过程通道共同作用；现有理论r过程产额对Sr、Y的估计偏高。

Abstract: The ancient stellar populations and simple structure of the local dwarf spheroidal galaxies (dSphs) make them excellent laboratories for studying the chemical evolution of the early universe. In this paper, we fit one-zone galactic chemical evolution models to C, Mg, Si, Ca, Fe, Sr, Y, Ba, and Eu abundances in the dSphs Sculptor and Draco. By including the s- and r-processes in our model, we are able to place constraints on the timescale of r-process enrichment in these dSphs. We find that neutron star mergers cannot be the only source of the r-process in Sculptor and Draco, and that a prompt r-process source is required to explain the observed stellar abundances. When neutron star mergers are the only source of the r-process in our models, their minimum delay time must be just 14-16 Myr. The neutron-capture abundances in Sculptor and Draco can also be fit by models with a longer neutron star merger minimum delay time combined with a prompt r-process source that operates on the same timescale as massive star evolution, such as magneto-rotational supernovae. Additionally, the Sr and Y abundances in the dSphs set limits on the Sr and Y yields per r-process event. We find that theoretical r-process yields overestimate Sr and Y production by a factor of up to 100.

</details>


### [90] [TractorJAX: GPU-Accelerated SPHEREx Forced Photometry and the Trade-offs of Regularization and External Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16007)
*Hyeonguk Bahk,Ho Seong Hwang*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 提出GPU加速的TractorJAX，测试正则化与外部流量先验能否可靠拟合超出SPHEREx图像深度约束的参考星表，发现两者均无法同时保持样本量与光度红移精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SPHEREx图像为欠采样，直接约束的星表深度有限。若将拟合星表扩展到更深的参考星表，能否借助正则化或外部流量先验获得可靠拟合，尚不清楚。

Method: 开发开源GPU实现TractorJAX，复现The Tractor的catalog-forced模式；在Abell 2537星系团场的模拟图像中，将拟合星表从m_z<21扩展到完整DESI Legacy DR10星表；并在9个SPHEREx观测场中测试，要求102个通道中过半通道S/N>5，评估可靠源数量与光度红移精度。

Result: 全深度正则化拟合在单张H100上处理100×100像素cutout耗时11.9 ms；扩展星表使分数流量残差散度增加34%；全深度正则化拟合的可靠源数比截断拟合少40%，共同样本的σ_NMAD大17%；外部流量先验源数接近m_z<21拟合，但共同标定样本的σ_NMAD增加0.0015。

Conclusion: 正则化与外部流量先验均无法同时保持截断拟合所达到的样本量和光度红移精度。最优拟合星表深度不能仅由图像层面表现决定，应通过面向具体科学目标的端到端测试来选择。

Abstract: We present TractorJAX, an open-source GPU reimplementation of the catalog-forced mode of The Tractor. We use it to test whether regularization or external flux priors enable reliable fitting of reference catalogs that extend below the depth directly constrained by undersampled SPHEREx images. For full-depth regularized fits, TractorJAX fits $100\times100$-pixel cutouts at 11.9 ms each on a single NVIDIA H100 GPU. In simulated images of the Abell 2537 galaxy-cluster field, extending the fitted catalog from $m_z<21$ to the full DESI Legacy Imaging Surveys DR10 catalog increases the scatter in fractional flux residuals by 34%, even with regularization. We also test the methods using SPHEREx observations in nine fields, assessing the number of reliably measured sources and photometric-redshift accuracy. Requiring S/N $>5$ in more than half of the 102 channels, we find that the regularized full-depth fit yields 40% fewer sources than the truncated fit. For sources meeting this requirement in both fits, the normalized median absolute deviation of the photometric redshifts, $σ_{\rm NMAD}$, is 17% larger in the full-depth fit. External flux priors recover nearly as many sources as the $m_z<21$ fit, but increase $σ_{\rm NMAD}$ by 0.0015 for the common calibrated sample. Neither regularization nor the external flux priors tested here simultaneously preserve the sample size and photometric-redshift accuracy achieved by the truncated fit. Our results suggest that the optimal fitted-catalog depth cannot be determined from image-level performance alone, but should be selected through end-to-end tests tailored to the intended science measurement.

</details>


### [91] [WALLABY pilot survey: accurate and automated linewidth measurements from global HI profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16057)
*Shinna Kim,Tobias Westmeier,Lister Staveley-Smith,Danail Obreschkow,Barbara Catinella,Nathan Deg,Karen Lee-Waddell,Jonghwan Rhee,Kristine Spekkens,O. Ivy Wong,Matthew Colless,Hélène M. Courtois,Boyi Ding,Bumhyun Lee*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文开发了一种基于BusyFit的自动化HI谱线宽度估计器，在WALLABY先导巡天中收敛率达95%，并给出参数不确定度与误差参数化，指出Wm50是最稳健的线宽定义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前FAST、ASKAP等巡天已提供超10^5个星系的中性氢谱与空间信息，未来下一代射电望远镜巡天样本量将再增数个量级，因此需要对星系HI发射进行准确、自动、可靠的参数化，以支撑动力学质量和Tully-Fisher距离等研究。

Method: 基于BusyFit谱线拟合算法实现自动化HI全局谱宽度估计器；用蒙特卡洛技术计算所有谱参数的PDF并给出误差参数化；比较多种常见线宽定义。

Result: 该估计器在WALLABY先导巡天中收敛于95%的全局谱；确认线宽不确定度主要依赖积分信噪比、谱线陡度与线宽，与前人研究一致；Wm50（平均通量密度50%处的宽度）是最稳健的线宽测量；并展示了基于WALLABY先导巡天的重子Tully-Fisher关系示例。

Conclusion: 该自动化估计器可可靠、高效地参数化大规模HI巡天谱线宽度，Wm50作为推荐线宽定义，为动力学质量与Tully-Fisher距离研究提供支持。

Abstract: Current-generation 21-cm surveys of extragalactic neutral hydrogen (HI) with instruments such as FAST and ASKAP are providing detailed spectral and spatial information for over 10^5 galaxies. Future HI surveys with next-generation radio telescopes could provide sample sizes larger by orders of magnitude. There is therefore a need for accurate, automated, and reliable parameterisation of the HI emission from galaxies. We have developed an automated estimator for HI global profile widths to better enable studies of, for example, dynamical masses and Tully-Fisher distances. Our estimator is based on an implementation of the BusyFit profile-fitting algorithm, and converges for 95% of global profiles in the WALLABY pilot survey. Probability density functions are calculated for all profile parameters using Monte Carlo techniques, and example error parameterisations are given. We confirm that linewidth uncertainties depend primarily on the integrated signal-to-noise ratio, profile steepness, and the linewidth, consistent with previous studies. We compare several common linewidth definitions, and the width at 50% of the mean flux density (Wm50) is found to be the most robust measure of linewidth. We provide examples of the baryonic Tully-Fisher relation drawn from the WALLABY pilot survey.

</details>


### [92] [Intrinsic Soft X-ray Emission from the Hot Circumgalactic Medium in IllustrisTNG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16200)
*Minjae Kim,Junhan Kim*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 利用IllustrisTNG的TNG100-2模拟，研究z=0、0.1、0.2红移下热环星系介质（CGM）的0.5–2 keV内在X射线辐射，发现其与恒星形成活动相关，且软X射线不仅探测气体含量，也反映热CGM的结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解热环星系介质（CGM）的性质及其与星系恒星形成和反馈过程的关系，探讨软X射线辐射能否作为探测热CGM结构的探针。

Method: 在TNG100-2模拟中计算0.15R500至R500球壳内温度T≥10^6 K气体单元的0.5–2 keV辐射，并按恒星形成活动对样本分类，分析X射线光度与晕质量、恒星形成率的关系，比较活跃与宁静样本的辐射分布、气体质量及热力学状态。

Result: X射线光度随晕质量增加；高活跃样本在扣除晕质量线性影响后仍与光度呈正相关，宁静样本相关性较弱。在10^{11.75}<M500/M⊙<10^{12.25}区间，高活跃样本辐射更向中心集中，中位光度约为宁静样本的5倍；但两类样本的热气体中位质量相近，说明差异源于辐射气体的空间分布和热力学状态（尽管样本未在晕质量上匹配）。

Conclusion: 软X射线辐射既能探测热CGM的气体含量，也能反映其结构，为研究恒星形成与反馈如何塑造气体晕提供了途径。

Abstract: We study the intrinsic 0.5--2~keV emission of the hot circumgalactic medium (CGM) in the IllustrisTNG simulation TNG100-2 at redshifts 0, 0.1, and 0.2. We calculate the emission of gas cells with $T \geq 10^6$~K in the spherical shell between $0.15R_{500}$ and $R_{500}$ and compare samples selected by their star-formation activity. X-ray luminosity increases with halo mass, although the class-dependent fits cover different mass intervals. Among galaxies with positive luminosity and star formation rate (SFR), the high-activity sample retains a positive association after a linear adjustment for halo mass. The residual association is weaker in the quiescent subset with positive luminosity and SFR. Within $10^{11.75}<M_{500}/M_\odot<10^{12.25}$, the high-activity sample has more centrally concentrated emission and a median luminosity about five times that of the quiescent sample. The two populations have similar median hot-gas masses despite their different X-ray luminosities and radial profiles. These differences are consistent with variations in the spatial distribution and thermodynamic state of the emitting gas, although the samples are not matched in halo mass. Our results suggest that soft X-ray emission probes the structure of the hot CGM as well as its gas content, offering a way to investigate how star formation and feedback shape gaseous halos.

</details>


### [93] [Galaxy Clusters from the DESI Legacy Imaging Surveys. IV. Alignment of Member Galaxy Orientations at z < 0.5](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16265)
*Zhaobin Chen,Yizhou Gu,Shufei Liu,Hu Zou,Qirong Yuan*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 利用DESI测光数据系统分析星系团中恒星形成星系(SFG)与宁静星系(QG)的空间取向，发现两类星系在BCG取向(BA)和卫星取向(SA)信号上呈现相反趋势，反映星系沿宇宙纤维输运驱动星系团演化的过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究星系团中不同类型星系（恒星形成星系与宁静星系）的空间取向差异，理解星系沿宇宙纤维输运及星系团弛豫过程如何塑造星系取向，进而揭示星系团演化机制。

Method: 基于DESI测光星表数据，系统测量星系团中SFG与QG相对最亮中心星系(BCG)的取向信号(BA)及卫星星系取向信号(SA)，并考察其与星系团外围/内部区域、星系团丰富度、QG比例、光度、轴比、卫星颜色、形态及红移等参数的关系。

Result: BA信号中SFG在星系团外围取向更强，QG在内部更强；SA信号中两者在外围近各向同性，在内部呈相反信号。BA信号依赖星系团丰富度和QG比例，在成熟星系团中更明显；QG整体BA信号更强，且随光度和轴比增强，而SFG较弱且无明显依赖。BA信号强烈依赖卫星颜色且与形态无关（红盘与蓝椭球星系分析证实）。BA与SA在z<0.5均无显著红移依赖。

Conclusion: 星系沿宇宙纤维输运驱动星系团演化：早期SFG沿纤维落入星系团，在星系团弛豫过程中其空间取向被引力势与潮汐力重塑，导致BA信号减弱、SA信号增强；而QG作为星系团早期成员，呈现更显著的各向异性分布并保持相对稳定的空间取向。

Abstract: Using DESI photometric catalog data, we systematically analyze the spatial alignment of star-forming galaxies (SFGs) and quiescent galaxies (QGs) in clusters. The results reveal that, in both brightest central galaxy (BCG) alignment (BA) and satellite alignment (SA), the alignment signals of SFGs and QGs exhibit opposite trends in several aspects. For the BA signal, SFGs show stronger alignment in cluster outskirts, whereas QGs exhibit stronger alignment in inner regions. For the SA signal, both SFGs and QGs exhibit nearly isotropic signals in outer regions, while in inner regions they show opposite signals. In addition, the BA signal depends on cluster richness and QG fraction, and is more pronounced in mature clusters. Specifically, QGs show a stronger BA signal overall, which increases with luminosity and axis ratio, whereas SFGs show a weaker BA signal with no significant dependence on these parameters. The BA signal also exhibits a strong dependence on satellite galaxy color, which is independent of morphology. We confirm this by analyzing the BA signal in two populations: red disk and blue elliptical galaxies. However, neither BA nor SA shows a significant dependence on redshift at z < 0.5. In summary, these findings reflect how galaxy transport along cosmic filaments drives cluster evolution: early-stage SFGs fall into clusters along filaments, and during cluster relaxation, their spatial orientations are reshaped by gravitational potentials and tidal forces, weakening BA signal and enhancing SA signal. In contrast, QGs, as early cluster members, exhibit a more pronounced anisotropic distribution and maintain relatively stable spatial orientations.

</details>


### [94] [Nearly pristine gas in a galaxy 1 billion years after the Big Bang](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16509)
*Sarah E. I. Bosman,Frederick B. Davies,Louise Welsh,Emanuele P. Farina,Prakash Gaikwad,Benedetta Spina,Barun Maity,Ryan Cooke,Klaudia Protušová,Valentina D'Odorico,Eduardo Bañados,James S. Bolton,Rebecca L. Davies,Xiaohui Fan,Martin G. Haehnelt,Joseph F. Hennawi,Tahlia J. Hill,Seyeon Hwang,Vid Iršič,Laura Keating,Girish Kulkarni,Samuel Lai,Emma V. Ryan-Weber,Lasse Seyberlich,Molly Wolfson*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 利用GHOST高分辨率光谱仪，通过背景类星体吸收谱发现首个中性氢气体金属丰度低于"超贫金属"阈值的星系UMP-DLA-1，其碳氢比仅为太阳的1/11000，刷新最原始星系记录，并证明其必然形成以超大质量恒星为主的星族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 碳、氧离子激发是坍缩气体云散热并形成新恒星的主要机制。在宇宙早期化学原始气体中，因碳氧匮乏，许多恒星质量远大于太阳。因此寻找超贫金属星系，有助于理解早期恒星形成与第一代或早期第二代恒星的化学产物。

Method: 利用新的GHOST高分辨率光谱仪，在明亮背景类星体前方寻找星系的光谱吸收特征，以此搜寻超贫金属星系。

Result: 发现首个含中性氢气体储库、金属丰度低于"超贫金属"阈值的星系UMP-DLA-1：碳氢比低于太阳11000倍，是此前最原始星系的四倍。它是目前唯一已知碳、氧丰度低到无法有效形成太阳质量及以下恒星的星系。

Conclusion: UMP-DLA-1的低金属丰度本身即保证其星族"顶重"（以大质量恒星为主），为早已消亡的大质量低金属丰度恒星留下的化学印记（升高的氧碳比）提供了直接观测。其丰度模式很可能接近第一代或早期第二代恒星的原子产额。

Abstract: Excitation of carbon and oxygen ions is the primary mechanism by which collapsing gas clouds dissipate heat to form new stars. At early cosmic times, in galaxies with chemically pristine gas, many stars are expected to have masses far larger than the mass of our sun due to the deficit in these elements. We searched for ultra-metal-poor galaxies using their spectroscopic absorption signatures in front of bright background quasars, employing the new GHOST high-resolution spectrograph. Here we report the discovery of the first galaxy containing a reservoir of neutral hydrogen gas with metallicity below the ultra-metal-poor threshold: with a measured carbon-to-hydrogen ratio 11,000 times lower than the Sun, it is the most pristine ever found by a factor of four. UMP-DLA-1 is the only known galaxy with measured carbon and oxygen abundances too low to enable efficient formation of stars with solar mass or below. The low metallicity alone therefore guarantees a top-heavy stellar population, providing a direct view of the chemical composition imprinted by massive low-metallicity stars that perished long before the present day: an elevated oxygen-to-carbon ratio. The abundance pattern of UMP-DLA-1 is likely a close representation of the atomic yields of first- or early second-generation stars.

</details>


### [95] [Identifying the principal mechanism of star formation feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16557)
*Chong Li,Rouyu Li,Keping Qiu,Marko Krco,Di Li,Hongchi Wang,Xin Lyu,Yan Duan,Min Fang,Xuepeng Chen,Lile Wang,Haoran Feng,Shuinai Zhang,Miaomiao Zhang,Shaobo Zhang,Yue Cao,Yao Wang,Yan Gong,Fujun Du,Zhiwei Chen,Yang Su,Yuehui Ma,Yuchen Xing,Yinghui Zheng,Tianwei Zhang,Shu Niu,Chenrui Li,Jiawei Liu,Zhijun Zhang,Yulian Guo*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 利用FAST在猎户座A发现心形膨胀的HI气泡，直接比较表明恒星风的动能贡献与辐射相当，挑战了经典反馈理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大质量恒星的反馈调控下一代恒星与行星形成并塑造星系演化，但长期以来缺乏观测证据来判定哪种反馈机制占主导。

Method: 对猎户座A开展大规模中性氢（HI）巡天，借助FAST五百米口径球面射电望远镜的高灵敏度与高分辨率，识别出一个包裹电离气体"Veil"气泡的心形膨胀HI气泡，并分别估算光致电离驱动与星风驱动气体的膨胀速度与动能。

Result: 光致电离驱动和星风驱动气体的膨胀速度分别为10.9和13.8 km/s；在球对称与均匀HI/H2比假设下，二者动能之比约为1:2。这是首次在单一气泡内进行的直接对比。

Conclusion: 辐射并未像经典理论预测的那样主导反馈过程，恒星风贡献了相当的注入动能，表明反馈机制需重新评估。

Abstract: Feedback from massive stars regulates the formation of next-generation stars and planets and thus shapes the evolution of galaxies, yet observational evidence capable of determining which mechanism dominates the feedback process has long been lacking. Here,we present a large-scale HI survey toward Orion A.With an unprecedented sensitivity and superior resolution, FAST (Five-hundred-meter Aperture Spherical radio Telescope) reveals a well-defined heart-shaped expanding HI bubble, enshrouding the well-known "Veil" bubble of ionized gas. The photoionization-driven and wind-driven gas expands at 10.9 and 13.8 kilometers per second, respectively, with a kinetic energy ratio of 1:2 assuming a spherical symmetry and a uniform HI-to-H2 ratio.This first direct comparison within a single bubble shows that radiation does not dominate the feedback process as predicted by classical theories; instead, stellar winds contribute a comparable share of the injected kinetic energy.

</details>


### [96] [Environmental Dependence of Satellite Planarity and Orbital-Pole Coherence around Milky Way Analogues in a Hydrodynamical Cosmological Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16710)
*Peng Wang,Yu Luo,Jiang Chang,Xi Kang,Shi Shao*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 通过TNG300-1模拟对比11922个银河系质量宿主，发现银河系经典卫星的扁平与共面性较为罕见，但当前宇宙网环境并未显著提升此类构型的出现率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验银河系经典卫星系统异常的扁平几何与轨道面共面性是否与银河系所处的宇宙网环境相关。

Method: 将11个银河系经典卫星与TNG300-1流体模拟中11922个银河系质量宿主里11个最大质量卫星对比；用简化惯性张量(SIT)与约化惯性张量(RIT)测量扁平度，用包含最共面8条轨道的轴的最小张角刻画轨道共面性；用2 Mpc/h平滑的速剪切张量描述环境，比较全体样本与类本星系群对、剪切本征值匹配系统及结节/纤维/片层/空洞宿主。

Result: 银河系SIT下(c/a,b/a)=(0.183,0.558)，RIT下为(0.364,0.736)，8轨道张角21.9°；模拟中至少同样极端的比例分别为4.94%、12.28%、5.04%。在采样充分的环境子集中，形状与张角分布差异很弱。

Conclusion: 仅当前宇宙网环境不足以显著提高银河系式卫星构型的出现率，但不排除各向异性吸积成因，因当前网类及其剪切本征值无法完整编码单个卫星系统的组装历史。

Abstract: We examine whether the unusual spatial flattening and orbital-plane coherence of the Milky Way's classical satellites are connected to the Galaxy's location in the cosmic web. We compare the 11 classical satellites with the 11 most massive satellites of 11,922 Milky Way-mass hosts in the TNG300-1 hydrodynamical simulation. We measure flattening with both the simple inertia tensor (SIT), which retains radial information, and the reduced inertia tensor (RIT), which isolates angular anisotropy. Orbital-plane coherence is characterized by the minimum opening angle containing the axes of the eight most coplanar satellite orbits. For the Milky Way, we find $(c/a,b/a)=(0.183,0.558)$ for SIT and $(0.364,0.736)$ for RIT, together with an eight-orbit opening angle of $21.9^\circ$. The corresponding fractions of simulated systems at least as extreme are $4.94\%$, $12.28\%$, and $5.04\%$, respectively. We describe the environment using the velocity-shear tensor smoothed on $2\,h^{-1}\mathrm{Mpc}$ and compare the full sample with Local Group-like pairs, systems whose shear eigenvalues match those reconstructed for the Local Group, and hosts in knots, filaments, sheets, and voids. Among the well-sampled environmental subsets, the shape and opening-angle distributions differ only weakly. Although the cosmic web imprints preferred orientations on host spins and satellite orbital-plane axes, the Milky Way combines orientations that are uncommon in the simulation. We conclude that the present-day cosmic-web environment alone does not substantially increase the occurrence of Milky Way-like satellite configurations. This result does not rule out an origin in anisotropic accretion, since neither the present-day web class nor its shear eigenvalues fully encodes the assembly history of an individual satellite system.

</details>


### [97] [Evidence for Hierarchical Turbulent Scaling in Nearby Star-Forming Galaxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16805)
*Luna Maria Scipio,Fernando A. N. Santos,Jay Armas*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 在分子气体速度中发现从云尺度到星系尺度的间歇性普适特征，并可用广义She-Leveque层级模型描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验湍流间歇性在分子气体中是否具有跨尺度、跨环境的普适性，并寻找其标度规律。

Method: 利用PHANGS-ALMA中31个星系的分子气体速度质心，计算至六阶的速度质心指数及其相邻阶关系；并与单指数标度及广义She-Leveque层级模型进行比较。

Result: 速度质心指数呈现非线性阶依赖，相邻阶指数近似线性；全部52个具有有效幂律拟合的星系和100个云中的99个都出现相对单指数标度的正偏移。

Conclusion: 广义She-Leveque层级模型能容纳这些特征，支持分子气体湍流标度具有跨环境的层级性描述。

Abstract: We find recurring signatures of intermittency in molecular-gas velocities from cloud to galactic scales. In 31 selected galaxies from PHANGS-ALMA, velocity-centroid exponents through sixth order show nonlinear order dependence and nearly linear relations between consecutive exponents across galaxies. Positive estimated offsets from single-exponent scaling occur in all 52 galaxies with a valid power law fit and 99 of 100 clouds. A generalized She-Leveque hierarchy accommodates these signatures, supporting a hierarchical description of turbulent scaling in molecular gas across these environments.

</details>


### [98] [Unveiling the Hot Interstellar Medium in Nearby Galaxies with eROSITA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16936)
*Roman Laktionov,Manami Sasaki,Elias Kyritsis,Andreas Zezas,Steven Hämmerich,Fiona Knies,Jörn Wilms,Frank Haberl,Martin G. H. Krause,Ashley Coombs,Philipp Weber,Aafia Zainab*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 利用eROSITA eRASS:4数据对25 Mpc内140个星系进行堆叠光谱分析，发现热X射线光度与恒星形成率在低至10^-5 M⊙/yr时仍紧密相关，而总X射线光度关系更平缓、离散更大，表明低SFR下未分辨X射线双星占主导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 星系弥散X射线辐射来自恒星反馈加热的气体与未分辨致密源，二者贡献需区分。此前X射线光度-SFR关系的标定多基于较高恒星形成率星系，对极低SFR星系及热成分的标度行为缺乏系统研究。

Method: 从Updated Nearby Galaxy Catalog选取25 Mpc内140个星系构成盲样本，与HECATEv2交叉匹配；使用eROSITA/SRG四次全天巡天(eRASS:4)数据。早型星系单独处理，恒星形成星系按比恒星形成率(sSFR)分为低/中/高三档。用热等离子体加幂律成分拟合积分X射线谱，对每个子样本进行光谱堆叠，导出总光度与热光度。

Result: 堆叠谱显示高sSFR样本软热成分更强，低sSFR与早型星系则由较硬的非热连续谱主导。恒星形成星系总0.5-8 keV光度满足log L_X ~ 39.5 + 0.9 log SFR，本征弥散约0.9 dex；热0.5-2 keV光度满足log L_X ~ 38.7 + 1.1 log SFR，弥散约0.7 dex。热光度与SFR的相关性在低至约10^-5 M⊙/yr仍成立，且与以往恒星形成星系关系一致。

Conclusion: 总X射线光度-SFR关系更平缓且弥散更大，说明低SFR下未分辨X射线双星很可能主导总辐射；相比之下热X射线辐射仍是恒星反馈的稳健示踪量。区分热与非热成分对正确标定X射线-SFR关系至关重要。

Abstract: Diffuse X-ray emission in galaxies originates from stellar-feedback-heated gas and unresolved compact sources. We investigate its thermal component and the scaling of X-ray luminosity with star formation rate (SFR), including galaxies with very low SFRs. We analyzed eROSITA/SRG data from the four all-sky surveys (eRASS:4) for a blind, volume-limited sample of 140 galaxies within 25 Mpc from the Updated Nearby Galaxy Catalog (Karachentsev et al. 2013), cross-matched with HECATEv2 (Kyritsis et al. 2026b). Early-type galaxies were analyzed separately, while star-forming galaxies were divided into low-, medium-, and high-specific-SFR (sSFR) subsamples. We modeled the integrated X-ray spectra with thermal plasma and power-law components, stacked spectra for each subsample, and derived total and thermal X-ray luminosities. The stacked spectra show a stronger soft thermal component at high sSFR, whereas low-sSFR and early-type galaxies are increasingly dominated by the harder non-thermal continuum. For star-forming galaxies, the total 0.5-8 keV luminosity follows $\log L_X \sim 39.5 + 0.9\log\mathrm{SFR}$, with an intrinsic scatter of about 0.9 dex. The 0.5-2 keV thermal luminosity follows $\log L_X \sim 38.7 + 1.1\log\mathrm{SFR}$, with an intrinsic scatter of about 0.7 dex. The thermal luminosity remains well correlated with SFR down to $\sim 10^{-5}\,\mathrm{M}_\odot\,\mathrm{yr}^{-1}$ and is consistent with previous relations for star-forming galaxies. By contrast, the flatter and more scattered total X-ray luminosity-SFR relation suggests that unresolved X-ray binaries likely dominate at low SFR, while thermal X-ray emission remains a robust tracer of stellar feedback.

</details>


### [99] [A Quest for Changing-State AGNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16982)
*Kasra Mokhtarpour,Vicki Sarajedini*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 利用Gaia DR3光变曲线与无监督深度学习异常检测，筛选出682个光变双态变化AGN（CSAGN）候选体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 识别光变上呈现"变化状态"的AGN具有重要意义，但传统方法依赖人工目视筛查，难以在大样本中高效开展，因此需要自动化、可扩展的筛选手段。

Method: 基于Gaia DR3光变曲线，对872,228个已知光变AGN采用无监督深度学习与异常检测识别异常光变模式；再用基于高斯混合模型（GMM）的自动统计模式识别，挑选星等分布符合两个优先亮度水平（双态）的源。

Result: 共8,504条光变曲线显示异常行为，其中682个源在Gaia DR3观测期内呈现双态光度行为。这些候选体相比非异常Gaia光变AGN样本，绝对星等更暗、红移更低，暗示部分可能吸积率较低。

Conclusion: 该工作构建了一个较大的CSAGN候选样本，减少了对人工目视筛选的依赖；后续观测可帮助揭示状态转变的驱动机制及其与吸积盘物理和超大质量黑洞供能的联系。

Abstract: We use Gaia Data Release 3 (DR3) light curves to identify photometrically selected changing-state active galactic nucleus (CSAGN) candidates. We employ unsupervised deep learning and anomaly detection methods to identify unusual patterns of variability in the light curves of 872,228 AGNs with known variability. We find that 8,504 light curves show anomalous behavior. Among these, 682 sources show evidence for two-state photometric behavior during the Gaia DR3 observing baseline. These 682 CSAGN candidates were selected using an automated statistical pattern recognition approach based on Gaussian mixture models (GMMs) to identify sources whose magnitude distributions are consistent with two preferred brightness levels, substantially reducing the need for human visual inspection in selecting the CSAGN candidates. The candidates tend to have fainter absolute magnitudes and lower redshifts than the non-anomalous Gaia variable AGN sample, suggesting that some may be accreting at lower rates. Our approach builds a large candidate sample with less reliance on human visual selection. Follow-up observations of these sources can help identify what drives changing-state transitions and clarify their connection to accretion-disk physics and supermassive black hole (SMBH) fueling.

</details>


### [100] [R(V) parameter of the dust extinction curve: mapping based on hot stars and connections to diffuse interstellar bands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17051)
*R. Lallement,J. L. Vergely,N. L. J. Cox*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过505颗热星的R(V)观测与Gaia XP消光数据、3D消光图及三组DIB（5780、5797、8620 Å）测量，研究DIB载体与尘埃颗粒的协同演化关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DIB载体被认为是随垂死恒星与尘埃颗粒一同抛射的含碳分子，但DIB载体与尘埃颗粒的并行演化及潜在相互作用尚不清楚。

Method: 汇总505颗热星的R(V)测量；反演基于Gaia XP光谱与近红外测光的消光数据目录，构建新3D消光图以定位每颗星R(V)对应的不透明区域；利用存档光谱测量5780、5797、8620 Å三个DIB；并将近期3D图的局部与视线方向R(V)同Gaia DIB强度图进行对比。

Result: R(V)在大空间尺度上存在变化，独立数据证实了已发表3D模式；Gaia DIB强度图与R(V)图有相似的大尺度变化，DIB载体丰度越高对应R(V)越高；在R(V) 2.3–3.4范围内LOS的DIB强度与R(V)正相关，但更高R(V)时无关联，可能因致密云中DIB载体消失；三个DIB的R(V)-DIB关系不同，从ζ型到σ型DIB呈由负相关到正相关的演化。

Conclusion: 该演化可用R(V)随消光双峰演化框架解释：ζ型（σ型）DIB载体在R(V)下降（上升）阶段消失；DIB强度与比值的多种组合可界定R(V)范围，但二者关系还取决于测定R(V)所用波长范围。

Abstract: Favored carriers of the Diffuse Interstellar Bands (DIBs) are carbonaceous molecules whose constituents have been presumably expelled by dying stars along with those of dust grains. The parallel evolution of DIB carriers and grains and their potential interplay are unknown. We assembled published R(V) measurements of 505 hot stars. We inverted a catalog of extinction data based on Gaia XP spectra and NIR photometry, and used the resulting new 3D extinction map to locate the opaque areas responsible for R(V) for each star. Using archived spectra, we measured their 5780, 5797 and 8620 A DIBs. In parallel, we compared local and line-of-sight (LOS) R(V)s from recent 3D maps with local and LOS Gaia DIB strengths from our recent map. Results: R(V)s from hot stars reveal variations on large spatial scales, confirming with independent data the published 3D pattern. Gaia DIB strength maps show large spatial scale variations similar to those of R(V) maps, with higher abundance of DIB carriers corresponding to higher R(V). LOS Gaia DIB strengths and R(V)s from 3D maps also correlate positively in the R(V) range 2.3-3.4, but there is no connection for higher R(V), likely due to the disappearance of DIB carriers in dense clouds. Despite averaging effects along the LOS to hot stars, we find differences in R(V)-DIB relationships among the three DIBs, with an evolution from negative to positive correlation from the zeta- to the sigma-type DIBs. This can be understood in the frame of the bimodal evolution of R(V) with the extinction, with the zeta (resp. sigma) DIB carrier disappearance in the decreasing (resp. increasing) phase of R(V). Various combinations of DIB strengths and DIB ratios may allow to define the range of R(V), but the relationship between DIBs and R(V) also appears to depend on the wavelength range used to determine R(V).

</details>


### [101] [Magnetic field evolution in feedback-dominated galaxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17216)
*Yue Samuel Lu,Dušan Kereš,Philip F. Hopkins,Sam B. Ponnada*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用FIRE-2磁流体动力学模拟系统研究了宇宙时间内磁场的演化，发现其经历膨胀、放大和饱和三个阶段，且这一过程具有普适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁场已成为星系形成模拟中的常见要素，它影响星系周介质气体并决定宇宙线传播，但其如何演化到观测到的微高斯量级仍不清楚。

Method: 采用FIRE-2磁流体动力学模拟，针对银河系质量星系和矮星系，分别在有、无宇宙线的情况下进行分析。

Result: 宇宙线对磁场性质影响很小，而晕质量控制z∼0时的饱和磁场强度；所有模拟均达到与晕无关的磁湍流比值约1-10%；磁场演化分为膨胀、放大、饱和三阶段，前末阶段由冻结通量主导，呈现幂律关系。

Conclusion: 磁场演化具有普适的三阶段特征，中间放大阶段可能由类似"发电机"机制驱动，并可能由湍流触发，受结构形成、反馈和气体湍流驱动。

Abstract: Over the last decade, magnetic fields (B-fields) have become a common ingredient in galaxy formation simulations. Although seemingly unimportant in matching observational constraints such as the $M_*-M_{\rm halo}$ relations, the B-field influences the circumgalactic medium (CGM) gas and determines cosmic ray (CR) propagation, making it an indispensable non-thermal component. Nevertheless, the intricate physics of B-fields, particularly how they evolve to the observed $μ$G-level field, remains largely elusive. In this work, we systematically address the evolution of B-fields over cosmic time using the FIRE-2 (Feedback In Realistic Environments) magnetohydrodynamics (MHD) simulations. We present results for both Milky Way (MW)-mass and dwarf galaxies, with and without CRs. We find that CRs have little impact on B-field properties, while the halo mass controls the saturated B-field strength at $z\sim 0$. However, all runs reach a halo-independent magnetic-to-turbulent ratio of $f_{\rm B}\sim 1-10\%$, though the methods used to measure turbulence could affect this value. We identify a three-stage evolutionary course for the B-field in tandem with galaxy formation: i. expansion, ii. amplification, and iii. saturation. The feature is universal, though the timings depend on the initial location and star formation history of the gas. Flux-freezing is the predominant driver for the first and last stages, exhibiting power relations $B\sim ρ^{2/3}$ and $ρ^{1/2}$, although the latter could also be explained by dynamical and energetic equilibria. The intermediate amplification stage, potentially driven by ``dynamos'', is fuelled by structure formation, feedback, and gas turbulence. We conjecture a numerically triggered, turbulence-driven amplification process to explain this phenomenon.

</details>


### [102] [Physical characteristics of the Galaxy defining the oscillation period of librating orbits near the OLR of the bar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17255)
*A. M. Melnik,E. N. Podzolkova*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 利用哈密顿力学的角-作用变量研究棒旋星系外林德布拉德共振(OLR)附近恒星的librating轨道，轨道伸长方向在有限角度范围内变化，振荡周期理论与数值模拟均为2.0 Gyr。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示棒旋星系OLR附近恒星librating轨道的动力学机制，理解轨道伸长方向变化如何调控平均轨道半径与角动量，并推导振荡频率的一般公式。

Method: 采用哈密顿角-作用变量；使用能很好复现Gaia DR3观测恒星速度分布的含解析棒模型计算轨道；对比理论振荡周期与数值模拟结果；推导OLR附近librating轨道振荡频率的一般公式。

Result: 理论周期与数值模拟一致，均为2.0 Gyr。轨道相对棒长轴倾斜方向改变时，角动量L与平均半径<R>随之增减。振荡频率与epicyclic频率之比 omega/kappa 由 q_R（棒径向力振幅与全星系平均径向力之比）和 <p>（librating轨道平均伸长）决定。

Conclusion: OLR附近librating轨道通过伸长方向变化调控平均半径与角动量，其振荡频率可由q_R和<p>参数化描述。

Abstract: We studied librating orbits of stars near the Outer Lindblad Resonance (OLR) of the bar using the angle-action variables of Hamiltonian dynamics. The librating orbits change the direction of their elongation relative to the major axis of the bar in a limited range of angles. To calculate orbits, we used the model of the Galaxy with an analytical bar which reproduces well the distributions of the observed stellar velocities along the Galactocentric distance R derived from the Gaia DR3 data. We compared the values of the orbital oscillation period of a typical star obtained from the theory and with the use of numerical simulation. Both values were found to be in good agreement and are equal to 2.0 Gyr. It was shown that the change in the average orbital radius, <R>, of a star, calculated over the time interval of one radial oscillation, is regulated by the change in the direction of orbital elongation. As the orbit is tilted relative to the minor axis of the bar in the sense of Galactic rotation, the angular momentum of the star, L, decreases, on average, causing a decrease in <R>. As the orbit is tilted in the opposite direction, <L> and <R> increase. We derived a general formula for the oscillation frequency, omega, of orbits captured by libration near the OLR of the bar. It turned out that the ratio of the frequency of orbit oscillations to the epicyclic frequency, omega/kappa, is determined by the parameters q_R and <p>, where the factor q_R is the ratio of the amplitude of the radial force generated by the bar to the average radial force produced by the entire galaxy at the distance of the OLR, and the parameter <p> characterizes the average elongation of orbits captured by libration near the OLR.

</details>


### [103] [Intermittency in the Multiphase ISM --- I. Extreme Density, Velocity, and Magnetic Fluctuations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17342)
*Yue Hu*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 该研究分析三个仅在压缩驱动比例上不同的理想MHD多相星际介质模拟，发现湍流驱动的压缩性控制间歇性在热相之间的分配，且密度、速度与磁场极端增量分别由不同物理结构承载。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 间歇性将强梯度、耗散和输运集中于稀有局部结构，是湍流的基本属性。在多相星际介质中，压缩与螺线运动的平衡可能调控极端涨落在各热相之间的分布。然而传统增量统计只能量化间歇性，无法识别承载间歇性的具体结构，因此需要将极端增量与局部物理结构关联起来。

Method: 采用三个匹配的理想MHD模拟，模拟热多相中性介质，唯一区别在于驱动的压缩分量比例。对密度、速度和磁场增量在各热相中进行统计。使用局部、尺度匹配的诊断量（压缩、热梯度、涡量、应变、磁旋度和磁张力）识别与极端增量相关的结构。

Result: 各热相中密度、速度和磁场增量均呈非高斯分布。增加压缩驱动使密度和磁场涨落优先重新分配到冷气体中，而非均匀增强所有相。极端磁场增量优先出现在磁旋度与磁张力同时增强处，且这种关联强于极端速度增量与其运动学宿主的关联；极端速度增量在涡量和应变中表现出相当的富集；极端密度增量仅在不稳定介质中优先追踪汇聚流和热梯度。这些关联在超过相依赖的相干尺度后减弱。

Conclusion: 湍流驱动控制间歇性如何在各热相之间分配，而密度、速度和磁场极端涨落分别由不同的物理结构承载。

Abstract: Intermittency is a fundamental property of astrophysical turbulence because it concentrates strong gradients, dissipation, and transport into rare, localized structures. In the multiphase interstellar medium (ISM), turbulent driving is especially important because the balance between compressive and solenoidal motions can regulate how extreme fluctuations are distributed among thermal phases. Yet conventional increment statistics quantify intermittency without identifying the structures that host it. We analyze three matched ideal-MHD simulations of a thermally multiphase neutral medium that differ only in the compressive fraction of the driving. Density, velocity, and magnetic-field increments are non-Gaussian in every thermal phase. Increasing compressive driving redistributes density and magnetic fluctuations preferentially into the cold gas rather than uniformly enhancing them across phases. We identify the structures associated with extreme increments using local, scale-matched diagnostics of compression, thermal gradients, vorticity, strain, magnetic curl, and magnetic tension. Extreme magnetic increments preferentially occur where magnetic curl and magnetic tension are simultaneously enhanced, more strongly than extreme velocity increments are associated with their kinematic hosts. Extreme velocity increments show comparable enrichment in vorticity and strain, whereas extreme density increments preferentially trace converging flows and thermal gradients only in the unstable medium. These associations weaken beyond a phase-dependent coherence scale. Our results show that turbulent driving controls how intermittency is partitioned among thermal phases, while distinct physical structures host extreme density, velocity, and magnetic fluctuations.

</details>


### [104] [Untangling Similarities and Differences in Star Formation Histories Across Cosmological Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17359)
*Alicia M. Savelli,Matthew A. Kustec,Joshua S. Speagle,Kartheik G. Iyer,Eshal Ahmed,Zehao Peng,Norman Murray,Cissy Kuang*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 利用约6.3万个模拟星系和7种宇宙学模拟，结合UMAP降维与自编码器，揭示恒星形成历史形状主要由少数几个平滑轴组织，多数形状为多模拟共有并可由暗物质晕组装解释，少数为特定模拟独有并反映子网格物理，且约95%可从长期形态推断来源模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 星系组装涉及从重子小尺度物理到宇宙学大尺度组装等多种过程，仅靠观测难以区分；因此需要借助大量不同建模假设的模拟，系统检验特定恒星形成历史（sSFH）如何随建模选择而变化，从而识别哪些特征由宇宙学组装决定、哪些源于子网格模型。

Method: 使用来自7种宇宙学模拟（涵盖流体动力学、半解析、半经验模型）的约63,000个模拟星系；采用非线降维方法UMAP构建二维嵌入以刻画sSFH形状变化，并用多目标自编码器神经网络从sSFH预测其来源模拟，同时进行消融实验以区分长期形态与短期变率的作用。

Result: sSFH形状变化沿少数平滑轴组织，大致对应峰值时间、峰值锐度和随后的淬灭速率；识别出多数模拟共有的“共同”形状，其一阶可由暗物质晕组装解释，以及少数与个别模拟绑定的“独特”形状，可能反映合并触发爆发等子网格与参数化选择；多数模拟共有一个快速淬灭星系群体，可能由活动星系核反馈驱动，晕组装无法再现；最独特的独有形状（Illustris主导、早期峰值后缓慢下降）来源尚不明确。自编码器以约95%的样本外准确率恢复sSFH来源模拟，消融表明该信息嵌入长期sSFH形态而非短期变率。

Conclusion: sSFH的形状变化可由少数几个与物理过程对应的轴来刻画，共同形状主要追随暗物质晕组装，而独特形状及快速淬灭群体揭示了子网格物理（如爆发与AGN反馈）的印记；由于来源模拟信息编码于长期形态，这些结果为连接恒星形成历史与子网格物理模型的理论与观测后续研究提供了方向。

Abstract: How galaxies assemble depends on processes ranging from small-scale baryonic physics to large-scale cosmological assembly, which are difficult to disentangle with observations alone. Using $\sim$63,000 simulated galaxies from seven cosmological simulations spanning hydrodynamical, semi-analytic, and semi-empirical models, we explore how specific star formation histories (sSFHs) vary across modelling assumptions. Using non-linear dimensionality reduction (UMAP), we find that sSFH shape variation organizes along a few smooth axes in a 2D embedding, broadly corresponding to (1) peak timing, (2) peak sharpness, and (3) subsequent quenching rate. We identify sSFH shapes that many simulations share ("common"), well described to first order by dark matter halo assembly, and a few tied to individual simulations ("unique"), which likely reflect subgrid and parametrization choices such as merger-triggered burst prescriptions. A notable exception is a common population of rapidly quenched galaxies found across most simulations, likely driven by e.g., active galactic nucleus (AGN) feedback, that halo assembly alone cannot reproduce. The origin of the most distinctive unique shape, an Illustris-dominated history with an early peak and slow decline, remains unclear, but we test the effects of short-term variability. With a multi-objective auto-encoder neural network, we recover the simulation that a given sSFH is drawn from with $\sim$95\% out-of-sample accuracy; ablation tests indicate this information is embedded in long-timescale sSFH morphology rather than short-term variability. These results provide avenues for theoretical and observational follow-up connecting star formation histories to subgrid physical models.

</details>


### [105] [Intermittency in the Multiphase ISM --- II. Non-Gaussian Dust Polarization and Foreground Variance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17390)
*Yue Hu*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 该论文通过三种热多相星际介质湍流模拟合成光学薄尘埃偏振，系统刻画了暖、不稳定、冷三相中偏振尘埃发射的非高斯结构，发现热相态与湍流驱动方式同时调节偏振尘埃谱及前景功率的场间分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 星际湍流会产生非高斯的银河系偏振尘埃发射，其高阶统计量依赖于热相态和湍流驱动方式。论文旨在厘清不同热相与驱动条件下偏振尘埃的非高斯特征，并评估其对宇宙微波背景偏振测量中银河系前景建模的影响。

Method: 基于三种热多相弥漫星际介质模拟（压缩、混合、螺线管驱动），合成光学薄尘埃偏振；利用E模和B模谱、单点分布、尺度依赖增量以及Minkowski泛函，对暖相、不稳定相和冷相中的非高斯结构进行表征，并与具有相同两点协方差的高斯场作对比。

Result: E模与B模均呈间歇性。暖相发射最接近高斯；冷气体表现出更平的中尺度谱、更宽的非高斯尾部以及偏离高斯形态最显著的特征。压缩驱动通常产生最强的投影间歇性，尤其在冷相和总发射中，而混合与螺线管驱动信号较弱。匹配两点协方差的高斯场低估区域间散布最高约2.6倍。

Conclusion: 热相态和湍流驱动方式不仅决定偏振尘埃的平均谱，也调控前景功率的场间分布，这对CMB偏振测量中的银河系前景建模具有重要意义。

Abstract: Interstellar turbulence produces non-Gaussian polarized Galactic dust emission whose higher-order statistics depend on thermal phase and turbulent forcing. We synthesize optically thin dust polarization from three thermally multiphase diffuse interstellar medium (ISM) simulations with compressive, mixed, and solenoidal forcing. We characterize the resulting non-Gaussian structure in the warm, unstable, and cold phases using $E$- and $B$-mode spectra, one-point distributions, scale-dependent increments, and Minkowski functionals. Both $E$ and $B$ modes are intermittent. Warm-phase emission remains closest to Gaussian, whereas cold gas shows flatter intermediate-scale spectra, broader non-Gaussian tails, and the strongest departures from Gaussian morphology. Compressive forcing generally produces the strongest projected intermittency, particularly in the cold phase and total emission, while mixed and solenoidal forcing show weaker signatures. These higher-order structures also enhance spatial fluctuations in polarized dust power: Gaussian fields matched to the same two-point covariance underestimate the region-to-region scatter by factors of up to $\sim2.6$. Our results show that thermal phase and turbulent forcing regulate not only the mean polarized dust spectrum but also the field-to-field distribution of foreground power, with implications for modeling Galactic foreground in CMB polarization measurements.

</details>


### [106] [Non-equilibrium chemistry and radiation hydrodynamics in metal-poor and metal-rich interstellar gas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17577)
*Shyam H. Menon,Piyush Sharda,Blakesley Burkhart,Tommaso Grassi*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个耦合非平衡化学网络（KROME）与非局部变爱丁顿张量辐射输运（VETTAM）的灵活框架，经基准测试验证后，应用于跨金属丰度与密度范围的1D/3D问题，揭示OH和H2O在低金属丰度致密气体中的冷却主导作用，并探讨湍流混合对PDR化学平衡及CI过量问题的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究需要更灵活地模拟非平衡化学网络与辐射输运的耦合，以准确刻画CO、OH、H2O、H3+等分子物种的形成与光致/宇宙线解离过程，并解决静态PDR模型在弥漫ISM中预测CI过量这一长期问题。

Method: 将KROME非平衡化学网络框架与非局部变爱丁顿张量闭合辐射输运方案VETTAM耦合；设计能够显式捕捉关键分子物种形成路径及光致和宇宙线解离的基准网络；通过基准测试验证，并在宽金属丰度（0≤Z≲Z⊙）和密度（n≲10^17 cm^-3）范围的1D与3D问题中进行测试；还比较了简化但更快的化学反应网络实现。

Result: 验证了方案的可行性；确认在Z≲0.01 Z⊙的致密气体（n≳10^5 cm^-3）中OH和H2O是关键分子物种并主导冷却，方案可达到与专用静态PDR代码相当的丰度精度；发现致密PDR（n≳10^3 cm^-3）中主导精细结构冷却剂随Z降低由CI转为CII再转为OI；在湍流介质中，PDR界面快速混合使化学物种偏离平衡，可能缓解静态PDR模型预测弥漫ISM中CI过量的问题。

Conclusion: 该耦合方案能有效模拟非平衡化学与辐射输运，适用于广泛物理条件，并揭示了湍流混合对PDR化学的重要影响；同时讨论了简化快速化学网络的性能-精度权衡及未来应用前景。

Abstract: We present a flexible implementation of non-equilibrium chemical networks with the $\texttt{KROME}$ framework, coupled to a nonlocal variable Eddington tensor-closed radiation transport scheme ($\texttt{VETTAM}$). Our fiducial network is designed to explicitly capture the formation pathways of important molecular species such as CO, OH, and $\rm H_2O$, intermediaries like $\rm H_3^+$, and the destruction of these species through photon and cosmic-ray dissociation. We validate our scheme through benchmark tests, and demonstrate its success in 1D and 3D problems covering a broad range of metallicities ($0 \leq Z \lesssim \rm Z_{\odot}$) and densities ($n \lesssim 10^{17} \, \rm cm^{-3}$). We confirm earlier works that suggest that OH and $\rm H_2O$ are the key molecular species in dense gas ($n \gtrsim 10^{5} \, \rm cm^{-3}$) for $Z\lesssim 0.01 \rm Z_{\odot}$, dominate the cooling at these densities, and demonstrate that our scheme can reproduce their abundances with similar accuracy as dedicated static photo-dissociation region (PDR) codes. We also find that the dominant fine-structure coolant in dense ($n \gtrsim 10^3 \, \, \rm cm^{-3}$) PDRs transitions from CI to CII to OI at increasingly lower $Z$. Finally, we find that in turbulent media, rapid mixing at PDR interfaces renders the chemical species out of equilibrium, and show that this could potentially alleviate the long-standing problem of (static) PDR models producing excess CI in the diffuse ISM over UV absorption-line measurements. We conclude by presenting and comparing with alternative implementations of reduced but faster chemical networks in our scheme, discussing the problem-dependent performance-accuracy tradeoffs and future prospects.

</details>


### [107] [Sulphur-bearing molecule survey towards prestellar and starless cores in the Perseus molecular cloud](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17556)
*J. Ferrer Asensio,S. Scibelli,A. Pokorny-Yadav,L. Steffes,B. Kulterer,Y. Shirley,A. Megías,I. Jiménez-Serra,A. Fuente,G. Esplugues*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 该论文利用Yebes 40m望远镜对英仙座分子云中15个无星核与前恒星核开展含硫分子（HCS+、OCS、H2CS、SO）巡天观测，给出柱密度并探讨其与核环境及化学演化的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 致密无星核与前恒星核气相中硫严重亏损，形成长期存在的"硫缺失问题"；观测含硫分子对理解硫化学、核-环境关系及恒星形成区物理演化至关重要。

Method: 使用Yebes 40m望远镜观测HCS+ 1-0、OCS 3-2与4-3、H2CS 1_{0,1}-0_{0,0}、SO 3_2-2_2跃迁，用高斯轮廓拟合，并在LTE假设下计算柱密度，同时与既往CS、CCS、CCCS观测比较。

Result: HCS+、OCS、H2CS、SO探测率分别为100%、93%、100%、73%；柱密度分别为(2.2-17.0)×10^11、(1.9-25.0)×10^12、(1.0-15.0)×10^12、(0.3-17.0)×10^13 cm^-2。HCS+与SO丰度分别与核体积密度呈强反相关与正相关，支持其早期/晚期类型特征；碳与氧化学存在分化，CS相关性最强；位于原恒星外流路径的核未显示一致偏高丰度；化学年龄(SO/CS)与物理年龄(体积密度)相关，Per752为例外，可能与增强的宇宙线电离率有关。

Conclusion: 结果为早期恒星形成中硫化学的化学模型提供了稳健的观测基线约束。

Abstract: Sulphur is drastically depleted from the gas phase in dense starless and prestellar cores, creating the long-standing "missing sulphur problem". Observations of sulphur-bearing molecules are crucial to understanding sulphur chemistry, core-environment relationships, and the physical evolution of star-forming regions. We present a survey of four sulphur-bearing molecules, HCS+, OCS, H2CS, and SO, towards 15 starless and prestellar cores in the Perseus molecular cloud using the Yebes 40m telescope. The HCS+ 1-0, OCS 3-2 and 4-3, H2CS 1_{0,1}-0_{0,0}, and SO 3_2-2_2 transitions were observed, fitted with Gaussian profiles, and column densities were calculated assuming local thermodynamic equilibrium (LTE). We also compare these with observations of CS, CCS, and CCCS from a previous study. HCS+, OCS, H2CS, and SO are detected in 100%, 93%, 100%, and 73% of the sample, respectively. Derived column densities are (2.2-17.0)x10^11 cm^-2 for HCS+, (1.9-25.0)x10^12 cm^-2 for OCS, (1.0-15.0)x10^12 cm^-2 for H2CS, and (0.3-17.0)x10^13 cm^-2 for SO, spanning over an order of magnitude. HCS+ and SO abundances strongly correlate (inversely and directly, respectively) with core volume density, reinforcing their early- and late-type status. Correlations indicate a differentiation between carbon and oxygen chemistry for sulphur-bearing molecules, with CS showing the strongest correlations as expected. Cores in protostellar outflow paths do not show consistently higher abundances. The chemical age (SO/CS) correlates with physical age (volume density), except for Per752, an outlier likely linked to an enhanced cosmic ray ionization rate. These results provide a robust observational baseline constraining chemical models of sulphur chemistry in early star formation.

</details>


### [108] [A radio counterpart of the local little-red-dot J092359+415736](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17559)
*Tao An,Ruqiu Lin,Ailing Wang*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 该论文研究本地小红点（LRD）J092359+415736（z~0.323）的射电对应体，探测到其长期射电变亮，最佳解释为中央黑洞吸积活动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高红移LRD通常射电与X射线信号微弱，无法进行多频段多时标观测；本地LRD可作为类比样本，提供高红移无法获得的长期射电研究机会。

Method: 在FIRST、LoTSS、RACS、VAST、VLASS等档案射电图像中，于光学/Gaia位置测量0.144–3.0 GHz、1994–2024年的流量密度，分析光谱指数与长期变异性，并排除其他射电起源。

Result: LoTSS中144 MHz以42σ探测到致密对应体；光谱指数α≈-0.30±0.11；FIRST(1994)比ASKAP(2021–2024)低约2.6倍，显示26年长期变亮；VLASS两个历元间3 GHz流量上升2.3σ；纯恒星形成需约200 Msun/yr，远超H-alpha上限3.3 Msun/yr。

Conclusion: 普通射电超新星与潮汐撕裂事件被强烈排除，折射闪烁需超致密核发射体；持续射电对应体最佳解释为中央黑洞吸积。该源在三个射电探测到的本地LRD中光度居中，且是唯一报道多年变异的源，为这一类别的射电多样性增加了时间维度。

Abstract: Little red dots (LRDs) are compact red sources at z>~4. Broad lines suggest accreting black holes, yet radio and X-ray measurements are usually weak. Local LRDs allow multi-frequency, multi-epoch radio studies that are inaccessible at high redshift. We search archival radio images for a counterpart to the local LRD J092359+415736 at z~0.323, quantify its long-term radio variability, and identify the plausible origin of the radio emission. Flux densities at the optical position are measured in FIRST, LoTSS, RACS, VAST, and VLASS products spanning 0.144-3.0 GHz and 1994-2024. A compact 144 MHz counterpart is detected at 42sigma in LoTSS, coincident with the Gaia position, and is recovered in FIRST, RACS, VAST, and VLASS. LoTSS and ASKAP give a spectral index alpha ~ -0.30 +/- 0.11. The source varies on multiple timescales: FIRST at 1.4 GHz (1994) is a factor of ~2.6 below ASKAP (2021-2024) and below the extrapolated LoTSS-ASKAP spectrum, indicating long-term brightening over 26 yr; the 3 GHz flux density rises by 2.3sigma between VLASS epochs 2.2 and 3.2. A star-formation-only origin would require ~200 Msun/yr, against an unobscured H-alpha upper limit of 3.3 Msun/yr, implying an extreme obscured starburst. Ordinary radio supernovae and tidal disruption events are strongly disfavoured by the luminosity and 30-yr recovery, while refractive scintillation would itself require an ultracompact nuclear emitter. The persistent radio counterpart is best explained by accretion onto the central black hole. Among the three radio-detected local LRDs, this source is intermediate in luminosity and the only one with reported multi-year variability, adding a temporal dimension to the radio diversity of the class.

</details>


### [109] [Mergers may not be the main channel for galaxy stellar mass growth at redshift $z\gtrsim5$: Predictions from cosmological hierarchical models and simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17575)
*Hao Fu,Mohammadreza Ayromlou,Francesco Shankar,Lars Hernquist,Andrea Lapi,Daniel Roberts,Feng Yuan,Maulik Bhatt,Evgenii Chaikin,Simona Gallerani,Luca Graziani,Antonio La Marca,George Mountrichas,Yingjie Peng,Haocheng Wang,Kai Wang*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文结合多种理论模型与模拟，研究高红移（z≈5–11）星系并合率及并合驱动的恒星质量增长，发现并合虽频繁但对宇宙恒星质量密度贡献不足10%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并合是星系增长的重要通道，但在高红移时期其相对于恒星形成的作用仍不确定，需要多模型比较来定量评估并合对早期星系质量增长的贡献。

Method: 在ΛCDM框架下综合DECODE半经验模型、TNG100与EAGLE流体动力学模拟、L-Galaxies半解析模型，并结合Millennium和TNG100-Dark暗物质模拟考察暗物质子晕统计与并合时标，比较不同重子物理和星系-晕连接处理下的结果。

Result: 纯暗物质模拟预测的并合时标比含重子的宇宙学模拟长约30%；主并合率随红移增加而升高，z~5约1/Gyr，z~8峰值约3/Gyr；但并合仅贡献总宇宙恒星质量密度演化的≲10%；z≳5时外源恒星质量占比从百分之几到约20%。

Conclusion: 宇宙黎明时期星系恒星质量增长主要由原位恒星形成主导而非并合；JWST识别的高双星系比例可能并不直接对应高效的外源质量增长，解释早期宇宙星系组装需要物理动机明确的并合时标。

Abstract: Mergers are a crucial channel of galaxy growth, but their role relative to star formation remains uncertain at high redshift. Here we investigate merger rates and merger-driven stellar mass assembly for galaxies with stellar mass $M_\star \gtrsim 10^8 \, {\rm M_{\odot}}$ at $z\simeq 5-11$, by combining complementary theoretical approaches within $Λ$CDM. These include the DECODE semi-empirical model, the TNG100 and EAGLE hydrodynamical simulations, and the \textsc{L-Galaxies} semi-analytical model, together with the Millennium and TNG100-Dark simulations used to examine dark matter subhalo statistics and merger timescales. This compares models with different treatments of baryonic physics and galaxy-halo connections. We find that dark matter-only simulations predict merger timescales $\sim 30\%$ longer than those inferred from cosmological simulations including baryons. Galaxy major merger ($M_{\rm \star,sat} / M_{\rm \star,cen} > 0.25$) rates are high at cosmic dawn and increase towards earlier epochs, reaching $\sim 1/{\rm Gyr}$ at $z\sim 5$ and peaking at $\sim 3/{\rm Gyr}$ at $z\sim 8$. Despite these high rates, mergers contribute only $\lesssim 10\%$ of the total cosmic stellar mass density evolution. The ex-situ stellar mass fraction ranges from a few percent to $\sim 20\%$ for all galaxies at $z\gtrsim 5$, across different models and simulations. Their broad agreement implies that stellar mass assembly at cosmic dawn is dominated by in-situ star formation rather than mergers. These findings suggest that the high pair fractions identified by JWST may not directly trace efficient ex-situ stellar mass growth, highlighting the need for physically motivated merger timescales to interpret galaxy assembly in the early Universe.

</details>


### [110] [Dynamical formation of dormant black holes: Predictions for Gaia DR4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18601)
*Daniel Marín Pina,Michela Mapelli,Giuliano Iorio,Cecilia Sgalletta*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用N体模拟结合双星演化，研究低质量星团中休眠黑洞双星的形成，发现多数通过交换通道形成，并预测Gaia DR4将发现数十颗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 休眠黑洞双星是研究星团动力学与双星演化对致密双星影响的探针。目前已知数量少、起源不确定，动力学形成是可能通道之一，且Gaia DR4将大幅增加样本，需理论预测。

Method: 使用N体代码PeTar与星族合成代码SEVN耦合，模拟单星和双星演化。网格覆盖星团质量300-4800 M☉、金属丰度1.4×10⁻²和1.4×10⁻⁴、以及有无fallback的原初kick模型。

Result: 几乎所有休眠黑洞双星在约1 Gyr（弱kick）或约10 Myr（强kick）内逃逸母星团；73%通过交换形成；轨道周期分布宽广（数小时至10⁸天），偏心率呈双峰，恒星质量遵循（演化后的）初始质量函数；大质量黑洞是动力学形成的标志。预测Gaia DR4含约1-64颗高金属丰度和约0.4-48颗低金属丰度休眠黑洞，周期峰值约10³天，>90%偏心率e>0.1，约20%大质量黑洞（m_BH≳30 M☉）。

Conclusion: 低质量星团中休眠黑洞双星主要通过动力学交换和碰撞形成，其性质（周期、偏心率、质量）受kick模型强烈影响；Gaia DR4的观测样本将可检验这些理论预测。

Abstract: Dormant black holes (dormant BHs, i.e., X-ray quiet binaries comprising a BH and a companion star) are probes of the effect of cluster dynamics and binary stellar evolution on compact-object binaries. A dozen dormant BH binaries are known, a number that will increase substantially with the upcoming Gaia Data Release 4 (DR4). Their origin is uncertain, and dynamical formation is proposed as one possible channel. Here, we study the formation of dormant BHs in low-mass star clusters, accounting for the interplay of binary stellar evolution and cluster dynamics. We present new simulations with the $N$-body code PeTar, interfaced with the population synthesis code SEVN for single and binary star evolution. Our grid spans a range of cluster masses ($300-4800\,$M$_\odot$), metallicities ($1.4\times10^{-2}$, $1.4\times10^{-4}$), and natal kick models (with and without fallback). We find that almost all dormant BH binaries escape the parent cluster within $\sim1\,$Gyr ($\sim10\,$Myr) when assuming weak (strong) natal kicks. Most ($73\%$) dormant BHs form via the exchange of a BH (or its progenitor star) into a stellar binary, or of a star into a binary with a BH. Dormant BHs in low-mass clusters have a wide orbital period distribution (from a few hours to $10^8\,$d), with an upper limit set by natal kicks, a bimodal eccentricity distribution (a first peak at zero eccentricity for circularised systems and a second at high eccentricity), and stellar masses that follow the (evolved) initial mass function. Large BH masses are a smoking gun of dynamical formation, via exchanges and stellar collisions. We expect Gaia DR4 to contain $\sim1-64$ high-metallicity and $\sim0.4-48$ low-metallicity dormant BHs, with a period distribution peaking at $\sim10^3\,$d, $\gtrsim90\%$ of them with eccentricity $e>0.1$, and $\sim20\%$ with large BH masses ($m_{\rm BH} \gtrsim 30\,$M$_\odot$).

</details>


### [111] [Phase Separation of HI in the Draco Nebula: CNM formation Triggered by Colliding WNM Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17753)
*Yuhi Naruse,Hiroaki Yamamoto,Yamato Matsuzuki,Rin Yamada,Atsushi Nishimura,Toshikazu Onishi*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 利用DHIGLS H I 21 cm数据与ROHSA分解方法，研究Draco星云中H I的热相结构与运动学，揭示从WNM经热不稳定气体演化到CNM的过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 厘清下落H I如何演化为冷原子云和分子云，并连接此前关于动力学相互作用与Draco分子云形成的证据。

Method: 使用DHIGLS H I 21 cm数据，应用ROHSA算法将发射分解为空间相干的高斯成分，并分类为WNM、LNM与CNM。

Result: 识别出两个运动学上不同的WNM成分（WNM1约-3 km/s，WNM2约-30 km/s）；LNM形成连接两WNM的宽速度桥，CNM集中于较窄中间速度范围并与CO发射关联。H I质量分数约为WNM1 41%、WNM2 23%、LNM 17%、CNM 19%，LNM与CNM合计约36%。中间速度处未发现独立的WNM成分。

Conclusion: 观测相结构与动态压缩的多相相互作用层一致，压缩提高密度、缩短冷却时间，驱动WNM向CNM演化；结果提供动力学相互作用与分子云形成之间的缺失环节，并将CNM形成确定为气体吸积转化为冷原子和分子云的关键步骤。

Abstract: We investigate the thermal phase structures and kinematics of atomic hydrogen (H I) gas in the Draco nebula to clarify how infalling H I evolves toward cold atomic and molecular clouds. Using the DHIGLS H I 21 cm data, we apply the Regularized Optimization for Hyper-Spectral Analysis (ROHSA) algorithm to decompose the emission into spatially coherent Gaussian components, and classify them as warm neutral medium (WNM), lukewarm neutral medium (LNM), and cold neutral medium (CNM). We identify two kinematically distinct WNM components: WNM1 near $-3$ km s$^{-1}$ and WNM2 near $-30$ km s$^{-1}$. The LNM forms a broad velocity bridge between two WNMs, whereas the CNM is concentrated within a narrower intermediate velocity range. CO emission is also confined to this interval and is associated with CNM-rich regions. The H I mass fractions are $\sim 41\%$, $\sim 23\%$, $\sim 17\%$, and $\sim 19\%$ for WNM1, WNM2, LNM, and CNM, respectively, with the LNM and CNM together accounting for $\sim 36\%$ of the total H I mass. No comparably independent WNM component is recovered at intermediate velocities, although a weak broad component may remain unresolved. The observed phase structures are consistent with a dynamically compressed multiphase interaction layer in which compression increases the gas density, shortens the radiative cooling time, and drives the evolution from WNM through thermally unstable gas toward the CNM. The present interaction may both create new CNM, and further compress or grow pre-existing dense structures. Our results provide the missing link between previous evidence for dynamical interaction and molecular cloud formation in Draco, and identify CNM formation as a key step in transforming gas accreting toward the Galactic disk into cold atomic and molecular clouds.

</details>


### [112] [Atomic hydrogen in $z=0$ galaxies in the COLIBRE simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18046)
*Kyle A. Oman,Ruby J. Wright,Andrea Gebek,Diego Dado,Katherine E. Harborne,Evgenii Chaikin,Carlos S. Frenk,Filip Huško,Claudia D. P. Lagos,Aaron D. Ludlow,Sylvia Ploeckinger,Alexander J. Richings,Matthieu Schaller,Joop Schaye,James W. Trayford*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用COLIBRE宇宙学星系形成模拟，在红移z=0处研究了星系中原子氢（HI）的性质，发现未针对HI校准的模型仍与多项观测关系吻合良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解星系中原子氢（HI）的性质是星系形成研究的重要方面，但HI观测关系是否能在不专门校准的模拟中被自然重现，尚需检验。作者希望评估COLIBRE模拟（包含详细多相星际介质物理）在未用HI信息校准的情况下能否再现观测HI标度关系。

Method: 采用COLIBRE宇宙学星系形成模拟，直接模拟多相星际介质，包含辐射冷却至约10K、与尘埃耦合的非平衡化学以及尘埃的形成、生长与破坏。主要分析(200 cMpc)^3、分辨率单元2×10^6 M⊙的体积；并通过改变不确定模型参数的小规模模拟以及不同质量分辨率（上下相差8倍）的模拟开展收敛性检验。

Result: 模型未用HI性质校准，但与观测的恒星质量-HI质量关系、HI质量函数、速度宽度函数、HI质量-尺度关系及（重子）Tully-Fisher关系吻合良好，仅在星系种群极端处有偏差。HI表面密度剖面整体真实，但在大质量星系外围存在可能虚假的环星系HI云。主要标度关系对模型参数变化最多只有轻度影响。跨8倍质量分辨率的收敛性良好，中位HI质量、HI占据分数及HI质量函数相当稳定，但最高分辨率模型在HI质量较高处将HI源数量高估约2倍。预测了低质量、低速度端HI质量函数与速度宽度函数斜率的上翘。

Conclusion: COLIBRE模拟在未用HI信息校准的情况下能较好重现z=0星系HI主要观测关系，显示详细多相星际介质物理的自洽性；但大质量星系外围虚假HI云、极端处偏差及高分辨率下的过高估计仍需注意，并预测了低质量端的潜在观测特征。

Abstract: We present an overview of the properties of atomic hydrogen (HI) in galaxies at redshift $z=0$ in the COLIBRE cosmological galaxy formation simulations. COLIBRE directly simulates the multi-phase interstellar medium (ISM) and includes new methods to follow radiative cooling to $\approx 10\,\rm{K}$, non-equilibrium chemistry coupled to dust, and dust formation, growth \& destruction. We focus on the $(200\,\rm{cMpc})^3$ volume with resolution elements of $2\times 10^{6}\,\rm{M}_{\odot}$. No HI properties informed the calibration of model parameters and yet we find very good agreement with the observed $M_{\star}$-$M_{\rm{HI}}$ relation, the HI mass and velocity width functions, the HI mass-size relation, and (baryonic) Tully-Fisher relations, albeit with some mismatches at the extremes of the galaxy population. HI surface density profiles are realistic, except at large radii around massive ($M_{\star}\gtrsim 10^{10}\,\rm{M}_{\odot}$) galaxies where we find a population of possibly spurious circumgalactic HI clouds, whose abundances and masses decrease, however, with increasing mass resolution). Using smaller simulations to vary some of the uncertain model parameters, we find that the main scaling relations are at most mildly affected, although this may not extend to detailed HI morphokinematics. Convergence across mass resolution levels a factor of $8$ higher and lower is good: the median HI mass and HI `occupation fraction' as a function of halo mass, and the HI mass function, are remarkably stable, although the highest resolution model overproduces HI sources by a factor of $\approx2$ at $M_{\rm{HI}}\gtrsim 3\times 10^{9}\,\rm{M}_{\odot}$. Upturns in the low-mass and low-velocity slopes of the HI mass and velocity width functions with potentially subtle observational signatures are predicted.

</details>


### [113] [Radio ghosts: Radio polarimetry study of newly discovered cluster minihalo candidates or radio tailed galaxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18099)
*Sam Taziaux,Christopher J. Riseley,Stefan W. Duchesne,Francesca Loi,Alec J. M. Thomson,Aritra Basu,Timothy J. Galvin,Dominik J. Bomans*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文报道了两个弥散射电源MKTJ0355-4237和MKTJ0355-4230的发现，结合MeerKAT射电、eROSITA X射线和DESI红移数据，研究其射电谱与磁场性质，支持湍流再加速机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 星系团中心区域的延展弥散射电辐射通常与非热过程有关，但其起源及维持机制仍不明确，因此需要多波段观测来厘清其本质。

Method: 利用MeerKAT L波段（1.28 GHz）深度观测进行谱分析和旋转测度合成偏振研究，并结合eROSITA X射线数据与DESI测光红移目录刻画宿主星系团环境。

Result: MKTJ0355-4237被确认为尺度约400 kpc的星系团级微晕；MKTJ0355-4230性质不确定，可能为微晕或射电尾星系。两者均呈陡谱（α≈-0.7至-0.8），向外围变陡（α≲-2.5）；均探测到偏振，中心偏振度约1%。MKTJ0355-4230关联一个未编目星系团，其X射线形态偏离最亮团星系，指示动力学活动。

Conclusion: 谱的陡化支持湍流再加速是弥散射电辐射的起源。

Abstract: Extended diffuse radio emission in the central regions of galaxy clusters is commonly associated with non-thermal processes in the intracluster medium (ICM), but its origin and the mechanisms responsible for sustaining it remain uncertain. We present the discovery and multi-wavelength analysis of two diffuse radio sources, MKTJ0355-4237 and MKTJ0355-4230, to assess their nature and investigate their radio spectral and magnetic field properties through radio polarimetric analysis. We use deep MeerKAT L-band (1.28 GHz) observations to perform spectral analysis of the diffuse emission and study its polarisation properties via rotation measure synthesis. These are complemented by eROSITA X-ray data and DESI photometric redshift catalogues to characterise the host cluster environments. MKTJ0355-4237 is confirmed as cluster-scale minihalo with extent of $\sim 400$ kpc, while the other diffuse source MKTJ0355-4230 remain uncertain and could be either associated with a radio minihalo or radio tailed galaxy. Both show steep spectra ($α\approx -0.7$ to $-0.8$) that steepen toward the outskirts ($α\lesssim -2.5$), consistent with spatial variations in electron ageing and re-acceleration. Polarised emission is detected in both systems, with fractional polarisation $\sim1\,\%$ in the centres and increasing in the outer regions of MKTJ0355-4237. MKTJ0355-4230 is associated with a previously uncatalogued cluster and shows an X-ray morphology offset from the brightest cluster galaxy, indicative of dynamical activity. MKTJ0355-4237 exhibits a sharply bounded internal radio deficit without a corresponding X-ray counterpart, within current sensitivity limits. The spectral steepening supports turbulent re-acceleration as the origin of the diffuse emission.

</details>


### [114] [Massive black holes in the first billion years with JWST](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18263)
*Hannah Übler*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文综述了詹姆斯·韦伯太空望远镜（JWST）对早期宇宙活动黑洞的新观测发现，探讨其多波段特性、与宿主星系的关系、质量测量现状及理论挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JWST使人类能够探测到更暗、更小且宇宙学距离更远的早期黑洞，其表现出的多波段特性和与宿主星系的关系与近邻大质量黑洞存在明显差异，这些观测结果对现有超大质量黑洞演化模型构成挑战，亟需新的理论约束锚点。

Method: 本文采用综述方式，系统总结当前对早期宇宙新发现活动黑洞观测特性的认识，梳理其质量测量的现状、与宿主星系的关联，并讨论开放问题与未来展望。

Result: 早期黑洞展现出与近邻大质量黑洞不同的意外特性，包括多波段性质和与宿主星系的关系，这些发现为黑洞形成与增长的理论模型提供了新的约束锚点。

Conclusion: 早期黑洞观测正在重塑对现今超大质量黑洞过去演化的理解，质量测量和宿主关系仍需进一步研究，未来观测有望带来更多突破。

Abstract: The James Webb Space Telescope has unlocked uncharted territory for the study of early black holes, enabling the detection of fainter and smaller black holes at larger cosmological distances than previously possible. Compared to nearby massive black holes, they are characterised by several unexpected attributes, such as their multi-wavelength properties or their relation to their host galaxies. These observational findings challenge our understanding of the past evolution of present-day supermassive black holes, and provide novel anchor points to constrain theoretical models of black hole formation and growth. This review summarises our current understanding of the observed properties of newly discovered, active black holes in the early Universe, discusses the status of mass measurements and the relation to their hosts, as well as open questions and future prospects.

</details>


### [115] [MUSE-DARK IV. Comparing Dark Matter Densities to Star Formation Histories in 375 intermediate-z galaxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18282)
*B. I. Ciocan,N. F. Bouché,J. Fensch,D. Krajnović,J. Freundlich,H. Desmond,A. Jeanneau,B. Famaey*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 该论文通过三维动力学建模与SED拟合，研究中等红移星系中暗物质晕内斜率与重子组装历史的关系，发现恒星星质量（而非恒星形成历史）是内斜率的主要驱动因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理论预期重子反馈会将cuspy暗物质晕转变为cored晕，但内暗物质斜率与星系恒星形成历史（SFH）之间缺乏直接观测联系，本文旨在量化这一联系。

Method: 分析375个0.1<z<1.43、7<log(Mstar/Msun)<11.2的星系，采用MUSE运动学的3D正向建模、质量依赖（DC14）和广义NFW（gNFW）晕轮廓的盘-晕分解，并用非参数SFH模型对UV到MIR多波段SED拟合推断恒星族群参数。

Result: 贝叶斯模型比较显示DC14轮廓对相当部分样本统计上更优；gNFW拟合的内斜率符合DC14预期并再现对恒星-晕质量比的依赖。偏相关系数与随机森林分析一致表明恒星星质量（或恒星-晕质量比）是内斜率的主要驱动因素，SFH相关量无独立预测力。Cored系统倾向低质量、更bursty、组装更晚；cuspy系统对应更高质量、更低恒星形成活动、更早组装。

Conclusion: 暗物质晕内斜率主要由恒星星质量决定，而非恒星形成历史，结果与恒星反馈诱导cored晕的理论预期一致。

Abstract: (Abridged) Baryonic feedback is expected to transform cuspy dark-matter (DM) haloes into cores, but direct observational links between inner DM slopes and galaxy star-formation histories (SFHs) remain limited. We investigate and quantify the connection between halo inner slopes and baryonic assembly histories in a large sample of intermediate-z galaxies. We analyse 375 galaxies at 0.1<z<1.43 spanning 7<log(Mstar/Msun)<11.2 using 3D forward modelling of MUSE kinematics and disk-halo decompositions with mass-dependent (DC14-based) and generalised Navarro-Frenk-White (gNFW) halo profiles. Stellar population parameters are inferred from multi-band Spectral Energy Distribution fitting of UV-to-MIR photometry using a non-parametric SFH model. Bayesian model comparison shows that the DC14-based halo density profiles are statistically preferred over gNFW for a substantial fraction of the sample. The inferred inner slopes from gNFW fits nevertheless follow the DC14 expectation and reproduce the expected dependence on the stellar-to-halo mass ratio, despite this not being explicitly imposed. Partial correlation coefficient and random forest regression analyses consistently identify stellar mass (or, equivalently, the stellar-to-halo mass ratio) as the primary driver of the inner density slope, a result that holds robustly even when the analysis is restricted to the gNFW fits. In contrast, SFH-related quantities (e.g. formation time and 'burstiness' indicators) exhibit no independent predictive power on the DM halo inner slope once the dependence on stellar mass is accounted for. Cored systems preferentially occupy lower-mass, more bursty bins with later assembly times, whereas cuspy systems are associated with higher stellar masses, lower star formation activity, and earlier assembly epochs, in agreement with theoretical expectations of stellar-feedback-induced cores.

</details>


### [116] [EMU detection of mysterious multiple component radio-continuum emission from nearby spiral Seyfert I type galaxy 'Sand-timer' (NGC 7213)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18285)
*M. D. Filipović,Z. J. Smeaton,B. S. Koribalski,R. Kothes,A. Ahmad,R. Z. E. Alsaberi,C. Bordiu,A. C. Bradley,E. J. Crawford,A. M. Hopkins,E. Järvelä,S. Lazarević,D. Leahy,J. L. Payne,S. F. ur Rahman,G. P. Rowell,H. Sano,M. Selby,S. S. Shabala,E. J. Stanton,S. Taziaux,K. Tsuge,D. Urosević,N. F. H. Tothill,J. Th. van Loon,V. Velović,G. L. White*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 利用ASKAP EMU巡天探测到近邻Seyfert I型旋涡星系NGC 7213（昵称"Sand-timer"）的射电连续谱辐射，发现中心超大质量黑洞延伸出的奇特"星系超级气泡"/AGN喷流，以及两处偏振强度图像中先前未探测到的千秒差距尺度结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探测并理解活跃星系核（AGN）反馈与喷流活动在近邻星系中的表现，通过高灵敏度射电连续谱与偏振观测揭示NGC 7213中心超大质量黑洞驱动的辐射结构及其可能的演化历史。

Method: 使用澳大利亚平方公里阵列探路者（ASKAP）的宇宙演化图谱（EMU）射电连续谱观测，结合偏振强度图像分析NGC 7213的射电辐射形态。

Result: 发现从中心超大质量黑洞延伸出的奇特辐射（"星系超级气泡"/AGN喷流）；并在偏振强度图像中发现两处先前未探测到的千秒差距尺度特征，可能为被射电连续谱总强度图像部分遮蔽的"重启"双极射电喷流，其形态类似FR-II型射电星系的弓形激波。

Conclusion: 这些结构很可能代表NGC 7213 AGN喷流活动的早期阶段；另一种可能情景是该星系核心为超大质量双黑洞（SMBBH）并发生潮汐瓦解事件（TDE），从而导致这些奇特的射电连续谱辐射。

Abstract: We present an Australian Square Kilometre Array Pathfinder (ASKAP) Evolutionary Map of the Universe (EMU) radio-continuum detection of the nearby spiral Seyfert I type galaxy NGC 7213 which we nickname `Sand-timer' due to its morphological appearance. We find intriguing, peculiar emission extending from the central supermassive black hole (SMBH), which we refer to as galaxy hyperbubbles and/or active galactic nucleus (AGN) jets. Finally, we find two previously undetected kiloparsec-scale features in the polarised intensity image, which appear most likely also related to the central SMBH, as restarted bipolar radio jets, though somewhat obscured in the radio-continuum total intensity image. They resemble the bow shocks often seen in Fanaroff-Riley Class II (FR-II) radio galaxies, and so we propose they most likely represent an early episode of the `Sand-timer's' AGN jet activity. An alternative scenario is that the core of NGC 7213 may be a supermassive binary black hole (SMBBH) undergoing a tidal disruption event (TDE), potentially causing the peculiar radio-continuum emission.

</details>


### [117] [EDGE-INFERNO: The chemical signatures of assembly history in reionization fossils](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18473)
*Eric P. Andersson,Martin P. Rey,Alexander P. Ji,Justin I. Read,Oscar Agertz,Alexander H. Riley,Stacy Y. Kim,Astha Chaturvedi,Jennifer Mead,Kaley Brauer,Mordecai-Mark Mac Low,Andrew Pontzen*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 通过EDGE-INFERNO星族级宇宙学模拟，研究发现矮星系的并合组装历史塑造了其恒星种群的化学特征：单前身星系系统呈现连贯的丰度趋势，而多前身星系系统则表现为多个独立化学演化轨迹的碎片化种群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 星系中的恒星种群是原位形成与吸积成分的叠加，既记录内部化学演化，也记录并合卫星星系的历史。作者希望探究星系组装历史如何塑造当今低质量矮星系恒星种群的化学性质，并检验能否用化学信息反推组装历史。

Method: 采用EDGE-INFERNO的逐星宇宙学zoom-in模拟，选取当今晕质量约1.4×10^9太阳质量的星系样本。在保持当今晕质量不变的前提下，系统改变星系生长过程，构造跨越连续组装历史区间的模拟。通过分析金属丰度分布函数以及单元素和多元素丰度空间，评估不同样本规模下对组装历史与不同成分的约束与恢复能力。

Result: 早形成系统主要在单一前身星系中形成恒星，恒星形成中心集中，化学富集连续；晚形成系统通过吸积多个前身星系组装，恒星形成碎片化于多个晕中并具有独立化学演化轨迹。单前身系统在丰度空间呈现连贯、明确的趋势；多前身系统则出现多个彼此重叠有限的独立恒星种群。金属丰度分布函数可为至少50–100颗成员星的样本提供组装历史的初步约束；多元素丰度分析能显著改善不同成分的恢复，但稳健重建仍需更大样本；完全区分原位与吸积种群可能还需运动学信息。多前身系统中吸积星在高金属丰度下也可有显著贡献；外部富集可使吸积物质在单前身情形下呈现与原位星相似的化学趋势。

Conclusion: 星系组装历史在矮星系恒星种群的化学特征上留下可观测印记，金属丰度分布与多元素丰度空间可用于约束组装历史及区分不同成分，但要完全分离原位与吸积种群仍需运动学信息配合。研究为利用化学观测量推断矮星系组装历史提供了模拟依据与样本量要求。

Abstract: In galaxies, stellar populations represent a superposition of both in situ and accreted components, tracing internal chemical evolution as well as the histories of merged satellites. We investigate how assembly history shapes the chemical properties of present-day stellar populations in low-mass dwarf galaxies. We analyze a suite of EDGE-INFERNO star-by-star cosmological zoom-in simulations of galaxies with present-day halo mass $\sim1.4\times10^9\,{\rm M}_{\odot}$. We systematically vary galaxy growth while preserving the present-day halo mass, engineering our simulations to span a continuum of assembly histories. Early-forming systems form most of their stars in a single progenitor, with centrally concentrated star formation and a continuous enrichment history.
