In contrast, our late-forming systems assemble by accretion of multiple progenitors with their own stellar populations, resulting in fragmented star formation across several halos with independent chemical evolution tracks. These regimes produce qualitatively different chemical signatures: single-progenitor systems exhibit connected, well-defined trends in abundance space, while multi-progenitor systems show multiple, distinct stellar populations with limited overlap. We find that metallicity distribution functions provide initial constraints on assembly history for samples of at least $50$-$100$ member stars. Multi-element abundance analysis significantly improves the recovery of distinct components, although robust reconstruction still requires larger samples. Fully separating in situ and accreted populations likely requires kinematic information. Accreted stars can contribute substantially even at high metallicities in multi-progenitor systems, while external enrichment can cause accreted material to follow similar chemical trends as in situ stars, particularly in the single-progenitor regime.

</details>


### [118] [Black hole spin-mass correlation and accretion disks at birth stage of dense star clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18505)
*Zacharias Roupas*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 综述气体星团中恒星黑洞通过吸积驱动增长与自旋加速的通道，指出该过程是致密大质量星团（如高红移原球状星团）形成的内在组成部分，并给出与LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA GWTC-5.0一致的预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究初始低质量恒星黑洞能否在气体星团环境中通过吸积残余气体实现显著增长与自旋加速，从而填补对不稳定质量间隙中的黑洞以及高红移致密星团中黑洞形成机制的理解空白。

Method: 结合半解析建模、动力学模拟与流体力学模拟，计算黑洞吸积星团中第一代恒星形成遗留的残余气体；气体角动量由黑洞自身弯曲轨道诱导，形成吸积盘。假设初始黑洞质量低于对不稳定性质量间隙（≲55 M☉）。

Result: 黑洞质量可增长至约10^2 M☉，在足够致密且大质量的原星团中甚至可达数百M☉，从而填充对不稳定性质量间隙；预测出自洽的自旋-质量关系：低质量（≲20 M☉）时自旋低（≲0.2），中等质量跨越全部中间自旋，高质量（≳70 M☉）时饱和至较高自旋（≳0.8）。在GWTC-5.0中找到支持该吸积驱动子群的证据。

Conclusion: 吸积驱动的黑洞增长与自旋加速是致密大质量星团形成过程的关键环节，其预测的自旋-质量关系与LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA观测数据相符，为解释质量间隙中的黑洞及高自旋引力波事件提供了统一图景。

Abstract: We review the accretion-driven channel of stellar black-hole growth and spin-up in gaseous star clusters, developed within the framework of our MSCA project "protoBH". Our detailed calculations involving semi-analytic modelling, dynamical and hydrodynamic simulations suggest that the growth and spin-up of initially low-mass stellar black holes is an integral element of the formation process of dense massive star clusters, such as the proto-globular clusters observed at high redshift. The black holes accrete residual gas, remaining from the first star formation event, and form accretion disks generated by gas angular momentum induced by the black-holes' own curved orbits. Assuming initial black hole masses below the pair-instability mass gap $\leq 55\,{\rm M}_{\odot}$, the gap gets populated with black-hole masses growing to $\sim 10^2\,{\rm M}_{\odot}$, and even reaching several hundred solar masses for sufficiently dense and massive proto-clusters. We predict a consistent spin-mass relation with low black hole spins $\lesssim 0.2$ for low black hole masses $\lesssim 20\,{\rm M}_{\odot}$, spanning all intermediate spins for higher masses and saturating to high spins $\gtrsim 0.8$ for high masses $\gtrsim 70\,{\rm M}_{\odot}$. We find evidence in favour of such an accretion-driven subpopulation of black holes in the GWTC-5.0 released by the LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA collaboration.

</details>


### [119] [Massive double-exponential galaxies with extended LSB emission: An IllustrisTNG exploration II -are they unique?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18576)
*Suchira Sarkar,Kanak Saha,Anshuman Borgohain*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 利用IllustrisTNG-100大体积模拟研究巨型低表面亮度星系（gLSBs）的混合形态特征，发现仅约11%的大质量盘星系呈现此形态，其外LSD盘占据独特参数空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大质量盘星系（恒星质量≥10^11 M_solar，z<0.1）的星系形成物理极具挑战性。近期若干此类系统的观测以及Sarkar & Saha (2025)基于IllustrisTNG-50的工作，促使作者研究具有混合形态的巨型低表面亮度星系（gLSBs）。

Method: 使用大体积IllustrisTNG-100模拟研究gLSBs的混合形态（中心Sersic核+内层高表面亮度指数盘+外层延展低表面亮度盘），并与观测中的典型盘星系及TNG100中的单指数gLSBs进行对比。

Result: 尽管模拟体积约为TNG50的10倍，仅约11%的大质量盘星系呈现该形态，与TNG50结果一致。内盘分量落在典型盘星系的中心表面亮度-径向标长分布内，而外LSD盘显著位于外围，占据独特空间。中位核球占总质量比约0.2，呈盘主导，与典型盘和单指数gLSBs相似。中位暗物质质量分数较高（约0.9），符合gLSBs特征。外LSD盘达到比单指数gLSBs更暗的中心表面亮度和更大的标长值。

Conclusion: 该类混合形态星系在本地宇宙中并不普遍（约11%）。研究结果有助于理解其跨宇宙时间的形成机制。

Abstract: The massive disk galaxies (stellar mass >= 10^11 M_solar ) in the local universe (z < 0.1) encode challenging aspects of galaxy formation physics. We have studied one class of such galaxies - the giant low surface brightness galaxies (gLSBs) that show a hybrid morphology - a central Sersic, an inner high surface brightness, exponential stellar disk surrounded by an outer, extended low surface brightness disk, using large volume IllustrisTNG-100 simulation. This is motivated by the recent observation of several such systems and an earlier work by Sarkar & Saha (2025) from IllustrisTNG-50. We find that despite using a ~ 10 times larger volume simulation, only ~11% of massive disk galaxies show such morphology, consistent with findings from TNG50. This shows robustly that such galaxies are not common in the local universe. We compared them with the typical disk galaxies from observations, and single-exponential gLSBs from TNG100. We show that while the inner disk components lie well within the central surface brightness vs. radial scale length distribution of the typical disks, the outer LSB disks lie significantly at the outskirt, occupying a unique space. Their median bulge-to-total ratio is ~ 0.2 making them disk dominated, similar to the typical disks and the single-exponential gLSBs. We find their median dark matter mass fraction to be high (~ 0.9), typical of gLSBs. The outer LSB disks reach fainter central surface brightness and larger scale length values than the single-exponential gLSBs. Our exploration will be useful in understanding their formation mechanism across cosmic time.

</details>


### [120] [Cosmic Evolution Survey: LOFAR 144 MHz imaging of the COSMOS field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18648)
*E. Vardoulaki,T. Shimwell,M. J. Hardcastle,L. Doukas,L. K. Morabito,C. L. Hale,M. T. Sargent,V. Smolčić,G. Zamorani,G. Gozaliasl,F. An,M. Aravena,A. L. Faisst,M. Vaccari,E. Treister,A. Finoguenov,P. Best,J. S. Kartaltepe,C. Casey,S. Jin,V. Mainieri*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文首次发布COSMOS天区LOFAR 144 MHz深场源表及源计数，覆盖2.6平方度，含1984个射电源，90%完备性达1.48 mJy，源计数低至约0.4 mJy，并与LoTSS DR3及VLA-COSMOS 3 GHz结果一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: COSMOS是被广泛研究的多波段河外天区，但缺乏系统的低频（144 MHz）深场源表和源计数，难以与其他频段及未来更深观测对比，因此需要建立低频参考数据集。

Method: 利用48小时LOFAR 144 MHz荷兰阵列观测，在2.6平方度内成图（角分辨率15"×6"，中值rms约100 uJy/beam），采用峰值S/N≥5进行分辨/未分辨分类与源计数分析；通过蒙特卡洛注入-恢复模拟（含展源模拟）刻画完备性、流量密度重分布和形态依赖的源恢复效应。

Result: 获得含1984个射电源的源表；90%完备性对应1.48 mJy；欧几里得归一化微分源计数测至约0.4 mJy，与LoTSS DR3 144 MHz及按144 MHz缩放的VLA-COSMOS 3 GHz计数吻合；约5%源在波束匹配范围内有多个3 GHz对应体；与VLA-COSMOS 3 GHz星表位置符合良好，中值偏移ΔRA=0.35"、ΔDec=-0.83"，约为合成波束尺度的6%，且无明显径向依赖。

Conclusion: 该源表提供了COSMOS的低频视角，可作为未来更深LOFAR观测及该天区多波段研究的参考数据集。

Abstract: We present the first deep, targeted LOFAR 144 MHz source catalogue of the COSMOS field, together with the first 144 MHz source counts in this field. The data comprise 48 h of observations. Over the 2.6 sq. deg. region adopted for the catalogue analysis, the final Dutch-array image reaches a median rms sensitivity of ~100 uJy/beam at an angular resolution of 15" x 6". The resulting catalogue contains 1984 radio sources; a peak S/N >= 5 selection is adopted for the resolved/unresolved classification and source-count analysis. We characterised the catalogue using Monte Carlo source-injection and recovery simulations, including dedicated simulations of extended sources, to quantify completeness, flux-density redistribution, and morphology-dependent source-recovery effects. The catalogue reaches 90% completeness at 1.48 mJy. The resulting Euclidean-normalised differential source counts are measured down to ~0.4 mJy and show good agreement with LoTSS DR3 144 MHz source counts and with the VLA-COSMOS 3 GHz counts scaled to 144 MHz, with ~5% of catalogue sources having multiple 3 GHz counterparts within a beam-matched search region. Comparison with the VLA-COSMOS 3 GHz catalogue shows good astrometric agreement, with median offsets of Delta_RA = 0.35" (+0.03"/-0.05") and Delta_Dec = -0.83" (+0.02"/-0.03"), approximately 6% of the corresponding synthesised-beam dimensions, and no significant radial dependence across the field. The resulting catalogue provides a low-frequency view of COSMOS and a reference dataset for future deeper LOFAR observations and multiwavelength studies of this extensively characterised extragalactic field.

</details>


### [121] [The Connection Between the Growth of Massive Black Holes and Their Dynamical Histories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18659)
*Lee A. Whitaker,Michael Tremmel,Angelo Ricarte,Priyamvada Natarajan*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用Romulus25与RomulusC宇宙学模拟，研究黑洞的原地（in-situ）与外来（ex-situ）生长，揭示欠生长中心黑洞的形成机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞通过吸积与并合生长，但其生长与动力学历史如何导致黑洞-宿主星系标度关系中的离散尚不清楚，尤其缺乏对"欠生长"黑洞群体的成因理解。

Method: 采用Romulus25均匀体积模拟与RomulusC星系团放大模拟，识别相对于同类质量星系中黑洞而言"欠生长"的中心黑洞，追踪其吸积、并合与轨道历史。

Result: 外来生长贡献随黑洞质量增加而增大，并合在最质量大的星系中愈发重要；星系团与场环境在外来生长重要性上无差异且总体很低。欠生长黑洞经历的并合更少，动力学历史复杂，长时间在星系内"漂移"或与另一黑洞"竞争"，或表现为"晚期进入"原本无中心黑洞的星系。

Conclusion: 黑洞组装以原地生长为主，而原地生长又受黑洞在组装星系中动力学历史的影响；长时间偏离中心会抑制吸积，促成z=0处欠生长中心黑洞的形成。

Abstract: Massive black holes (MBHs) grow through both gas accretion and mergers and it remains unclear how variations to MBH growth and dynamical histories contribute to the spread in empirical scaling relations between MBHs and their host galaxies. We examine the ex- and in-situ growth of MBHs within large-scale cosmological simulations and compare the evolution of an outlier population of under-grown MBHs to the general population. We use the Romulus25 uniform volume cosmological simulation and the RomulusC cluster zoom-in simulation to examine the growth and merger histories of MBHs. We identify central MBHs that are undergrown relative to other MBHs in similar mass galaxies and investigate their accretion, merger, and orbital histories within the simulations. The contribution of ex-situ growth increases with MBH mass, with mergers becoming increasingly important in the most massive galaxies. We find no difference between cluster and field environments in terms of the importance of ex-situ growth, which is generally very low. Under-grown MBHs have experienced fewer mergers and have had complex dynamical histories, spending significant time 'drifting' within the galaxy or 'competing' with another MBH. Other under-grown MBHs have experienced a 'late entrance' into a galaxy which until then had no central MBH. Our results demonstrate that MBH assembly is dominated by in-situ growth, which in turn is impacted by the dynamical histories of MBHs within assembling galaxies. Prolonged off-centre evolution can suppress accretion and contribute to the formation of under-grown central MBHs at $z=0$.

</details>
